// Hand-inked strokes for paper pages (the baby-facts video, "Replicate (n = 1)").
//
// A stroke is a filled ribbon along a polyline. Its points wobble perpendicular to the
// stroke by smooth value noise of the arclength, seeded by the pen's seed and the stroke's
// key; its width breathes a little and tapers at the ends, as a technical pen does. Nothing
// depends on wall time or on how much of the stroke is drawn: o.p < 1 draws an exact prefix
// of the finished stroke, so a stroke grows in time without wiggling.
//
//   const pen = makePen(ctx, 'feldman', { color: '#1d1a17', width: 3 });
//   pen.line(x0, y0, x1, y1, { p })            p = drawn fraction 0..1 (default 1)
//   pen.poly([[x, y], ...], { close })         pen.spline(points)  (Catmull-Rom through them)
//   pen.rect(x, y, w, h)  pen.rrect(x, y, w, h, r)  pen.ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry)  pen.circle(cx, cy, r)
//   pen.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1)  pen.curve(p0, c1, c2, p1)  pen.quad(p0, c, p1)  pen.arrow(x0, y0, x1, y1)
//   pen.hatch(region, { angle, gap, p })       region: [x, y, w, h] | polygon [[x, y], ...] | (ctx) => path, with o.bbox
//   pen.dotted(points, { gap, r })  pen.dashed(points, { dash, gap })
//   pen.with({ color, width, ... })            same seed and stroke counter, other defaults
//   pen.sub('label')                           an independent pen for one element (its own seed)
//
// Stroke keys: by default each call takes the next index of the pen's counter, so the n-th
// stroke of a plate always wobbles the same way. Strokes that come and go (an element that
// appears halfway through a figure) shift the index of every stroke after them; give those
// a stable key (o.k = 'label' or a number) or draw them with their own pen.sub('label').
//
// Options (pen defaults, overridable per stroke): color, width (design px), alpha, wobble
// (amplitude, px), wave (wavelength, px), jitter (end-point slop, px), taper (end width as a
// fraction of the width), over (overshoot past the end points, px), step (resampling, px).

import { clamp, lerp, noise1, hash, shash, strSeed, TAU } from './util.js';

export const INK_DEFAULTS = { color: '#1d1a17', width: 3, alpha: 1, wobble: 2.6, wave: 150, jitter: 1.4, taper: 0.45, over: 0, step: 5 };

const seedOf = (x) => (typeof x === 'number' ? (x >>> 0) : strSeed(String(x)));
const mixSeed = (a, b) => (Math.imul((a ^ seedOf(b)) >>> 0, 2654435761) ^ (seedOf(b) * 40503)) >>> 0;

/** Resample a polyline: keep every vertex, insert points so no gap exceeds `step`. */
function resample(pts, step) {
  const out = [pts[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const [ax, ay] = pts[i - 1], [bx, by] = pts[i];
    const d = Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay);
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(d / step));
    for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) out.push([ax + ((bx - ax) * k) / n, ay + ((by - ay) * k) / n]);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Extend the first and last segments by `a` and `b` px. */
function extend(pts, a, b) {
  if (pts.length < 2 || (!a && !b)) return pts;
  const out = pts.slice();
  const ext = (p, q, e) => { const d = Math.hypot(p[0] - q[0], p[1] - q[1]) || 1; return [p[0] + ((p[0] - q[0]) * e) / d, p[1] + ((p[1] - q[1]) * e) / d]; };
  if (a) out[0] = ext(pts[0], pts[1], a);
  if (b) out[out.length - 1] = ext(pts.at(-1), pts.at(-2), b);
  return out;
}

/**
 * Draw one stroke along `pts` with options `o` (all of them resolved) and seed `sd`.
 * The geometry is a pure function of (pts, o minus p, sd); o.p only truncates it.
 */
function drawStroke(ctx, pts, o, sd) {
  if (!pts || pts.length < 2) return;
  const p = clamp(o.p ?? 1);
  if (p <= 0 || o.alpha <= 0) return;
  // end-point slop: a hand never lands exactly on the mark
  const j = o.jitter;
  let P = pts.map((q) => [q[0], q[1]]);
  if (j && !o.closed) {
    P[0] = [P[0][0] + shash(sd, 11) * j, P[0][1] + shash(sd, 12) * j];
    P[P.length - 1] = [P.at(-1)[0] + shash(sd, 13) * j, P.at(-1)[1] + shash(sd, 14) * j];
  }
  P = extend(P, o.over ? o.over * (0.6 + 0.8 * hash(sd, 15)) : 0, o.over ? o.over * (0.6 + 0.8 * hash(sd, 16)) : 0);
  P = resample(P, o.step);
  const n = P.length;
  const s = new Float64Array(n);
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) s[i] = s[i - 1] + Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]);
  const S = s[n - 1];
  if (S < 0.5) return;
  // normals (averaged at vertices, miter-corrected so the ribbon keeps its width at corners)
  const nx = new Float64Array(n), ny = new Float64Array(n), nk = new Float64Array(n);
  const segN = (i) => { const dx = P[i + 1][0] - P[i][0], dy = P[i + 1][1] - P[i][1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1; return [-dy / d, dx / d]; };
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const a = i > 0 ? segN(i - 1) : segN(0), b = i < n - 1 ? segN(i) : segN(n - 2);
    let x = a[0] + b[0], y = a[1] + b[1];
    const d = Math.hypot(x, y);
    if (d < 1e-6) { x = b[0]; y = b[1]; } else { x /= d; y /= d; }
    nx[i] = x; ny[i] = y;
    nk[i] = 1 / Math.max(0.55, x * b[0] + y * b[1]);
  }
  // wobble (scaled down on short strokes) and width
  const A = o.wobble * clamp(S / 80, 0.25, 1);
  const ph = hash(sd, 21) * 50, ph2 = hash(sd, 22) * 50, ph3 = hash(sd, 23) * 50;
  const end = Math.min(10, S * 0.25);
  const L = [], R = [];
  const sMax = p * S;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    let si = s[i], x = P[i][0], y = P[i][1], k = i;
    if (si > sMax) {
      // the truncation point, interpolated on segment i-1 -> i
      const f = (sMax - s[i - 1]) / Math.max(1e-9, si - s[i - 1]);
      x = lerp(P[i - 1][0], x, f); y = lerp(P[i - 1][1], y, f); si = sMax; k = i - 1;
    }
    const off = A * (0.8 * noise1(si / o.wave + ph, sd) + 0.28 * noise1(si / (o.wave * 0.23) + ph2, sd + 7));
    const ends = Math.min(1, si / end, (S - si) / end);
    const w = o.width * (1 + 0.16 * noise1(si / 70 + ph3, sd + 3)) * lerp(o.taper, 1, Math.sqrt(clamp(ends))) * 0.5;
    const cx = x + nx[k] * off, cy = y + ny[k] * off;
    L.push([cx + nx[k] * w * nk[k], cy + ny[k] * w * nk[k]]);
    R.push([cx - nx[k] * w * nk[k], cy - ny[k] * w * nk[k]]);
    if (si >= sMax) break;
  }
  if (L.length < 2) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= o.alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(L[0][0], L[0][1]);
  for (let i = 1; i < L.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(L[i][0], L[i][1]);
  for (let i = R.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) ctx.lineTo(R[i][0], R[i][1]);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

function ellipsePts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1, rot = 0) {
  const n = Math.max(12, Math.ceil((Math.abs(a1 - a0) * Math.max(rx, ry)) / 6));
  const out = [];
  const c = Math.cos(rot), sn = Math.sin(rot);
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const a = lerp(a0, a1, i / n), x = Math.cos(a) * rx, y = Math.sin(a) * ry;
    out.push([cx + x * c - y * sn, cy + x * sn + y * c]);
  }
  return out;
}

function bezierPts(p0, c1, c2, p1) {
  const len = Math.hypot(c1[0] - p0[0], c1[1] - p0[1]) + Math.hypot(c2[0] - c1[0], c2[1] - c1[1]) + Math.hypot(p1[0] - c2[0], p1[1] - c2[1]);
  const n = Math.max(8, Math.ceil(len / 6));
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const t = i / n, u = 1 - t;
    out.push([u * u * u * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * c1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * c2[0] + t * t * t * p1[0],
      u * u * u * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * c1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * c2[1] + t * t * t * p1[1]]);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Catmull-Rom spline through the points (a smooth hand curve through data points). */
export function splinePts(pts, k = 8) {
  if (pts.length < 3) return pts.slice();
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const p0 = pts[Math.max(0, i - 1)], p1 = pts[i], p2 = pts[i + 1], p3 = pts[Math.min(pts.length - 1, i + 2)];
    for (let j = 0; j < k; j++) {
      const t = j / k, t2 = t * t, t3 = t2 * t;
      out.push([0.5 * (2 * p1[0] + (-p0[0] + p2[0]) * t + (2 * p0[0] - 5 * p1[0] + 4 * p2[0] - p3[0]) * t2 + (-p0[0] + 3 * p1[0] - 3 * p2[0] + p3[0]) * t3),
        0.5 * (2 * p1[1] + (-p0[1] + p2[1]) * t + (2 * p0[1] - 5 * p1[1] + 4 * p2[1] - p3[1]) * t2 + (-p0[1] + 3 * p1[1] - 3 * p2[1] + p3[1]) * t3)]);
    }
  }
  out.push(pts.at(-1));
  return out;
}

/** Split a fraction p over pieces of the given lengths, drawn one after the other. */
function seqP(p, lens) {
  const tot = lens.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  let acc = 0;
  return lens.map((l) => { const v = clamp((p * tot - acc) / (l || 1e-9)); acc += l; return v; });
}
const plen = (pts) => { let d = 0; for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) d += Math.hypot(pts[i][0] - pts[i - 1][0], pts[i][1] - pts[i - 1][1]); return d; };

export function makePen(ctx, seed = 0, opts = {}, counter = { n: 0 }) {
  const base = { ...INK_DEFAULTS, ...opts };
  const s0 = seedOf(seed);
  const key = (o) => mixSeed(s0, o.k != null ? `k:${o.k}` : counter.n++);
  const res = (o) => ({ ...base, ...o });
  const pen = {
    ctx, seed: s0, opts: base,
    with(o2) { return makePen(ctx, s0, { ...base, ...o2 }, counter); },
    sub(label) { return makePen(ctx, mixSeed(s0, `sub:${label}`), base); },
    /** The core: one stroke along a polyline. */
    stroke(pts, o = {}) { const r = res(o); drawStroke(ctx, r.close ? [...pts, pts[0]] : pts, r, key(o)); return pen; },
    line(x0, y0, x1, y1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke([[x0, y0], [x1, y1]], o); },
    poly(pts, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(pts, o); },
    spline(pts, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(splinePts(pts, o.smooth ?? 8), o); },
    curve(p0, c1, c2, p1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(bezierPts(p0, c1, c2, p1), o); },
    quad(p0, c, p1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(bezierPts(p0, [p0[0] + (2 / 3) * (c[0] - p0[0]), p0[1] + (2 / 3) * (c[1] - p0[1])], [p1[0] + (2 / 3) * (c[0] - p1[0]), p1[1] + (2 / 3) * (c[1] - p1[1])], p1), o); },
    arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(ellipsePts(cx, cy, r, r, a0, a1), o); },
    /** A hand-drawn ellipse: starts at a seeded angle and overshoots its start a little. */
    ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, o = {}) {
      const sd = key(o);
      const a0 = (o.start ?? hash(sd, 31) * TAU), sweep = TAU + (o.overshoot ?? 0.18);
      drawStroke(ctx, ellipsePts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a0 + sweep, o.rot || 0), { ...res(o), closed: true, jitter: 0 }, sd);
      return pen;
    },
    circle(cx, cy, r, o = {}) { return pen.ellipse(cx, cy, r, r, o); },
    /** Four strokes, each overshooting its corner a little (drafting style); drawn clockwise in sequence. */
    rect(x, y, w, h, o = {}) {
      const sides = [[[x, y], [x + w, y]], [[x + w, y], [x + w, y + h]], [[x + w, y + h], [x, y + h]], [[x, y + h], [x, y]]];
      const ps = seqP(o.p ?? 1, [w, h, w, h]);
      const over = o.over ?? 3;
      sides.forEach((sd, i) => pen.stroke(sd, { ...o, p: ps[i], over, k: o.k != null ? `${o.k}:${i}` : undefined }));
      return pen;
    },
    rrect(x, y, w, h, r, o = {}) {
      r = Math.max(0, Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2));
      const pts = [];
      const corner = (cx, cy, a0) => { for (const q of ellipsePts(cx, cy, r, r, a0, a0 + Math.PI / 2)) pts.push(q); };
      pts.push([x + r, y]);
      corner(x + w - r, y + r, -Math.PI / 2);
      corner(x + w - r, y + h - r, 0);
      corner(x + r, y + h - r, Math.PI / 2);
      corner(x + r, y + r, Math.PI);
      pts.push([x + r + 2, y]);
      return pen.stroke(pts, { ...o, jitter: 0 });
    },
    arrow(x0, y0, x1, y1, o = {}) {
      const p = o.p ?? 1;
      const L = Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) || 1, ux = (x1 - x0) / L, uy = (y1 - y0) / L;
      const head = o.head ?? Math.min(18, L * 0.3);
      const [pa, pb] = seqP(p, [L, head * 1.6]);
      pen.line(x0, y0, x1, y1, { ...o, p: pa });
      if (pb > 0) for (const sgn of [-1, 1]) {
        const a = sgn * 0.45;
        const hx = x1 - (ux * Math.cos(a) - uy * Math.sin(a)) * head, hy = y1 - (uy * Math.cos(a) + ux * Math.sin(a)) * head;
        pen.line(x1, y1, hx, hy, { ...o, p: pb, k: o.k != null ? `${o.k}:h${sgn}` : undefined });
      }
      return pen;
    },
    /**
     * Hatching: parallel thin strokes clipped to a region. region = [x, y, w, h], a polygon
     * [[x, y], ...] or a function that builds a path on ctx (then pass o.bbox = [x, y, w, h]).
     * o.angle (radians, default -45 deg), o.gap (px), o.p draws the lines in one by one.
     */
    hatch(region, o = {}) {
      const r = res({ width: Math.max(1, base.width * 0.45), wobble: base.wobble * 0.35, jitter: 2, ...o });
      let bbox = o.bbox, path;
      if (typeof region === 'function') path = region;
      else if (region.length === 4 && typeof region[0] === 'number') { bbox = region; path = (c) => { c.beginPath(); c.rect(region[0], region[1], region[2], region[3]); }; }
      else {
        const xs = region.map((q) => q[0]), ys = region.map((q) => q[1]);
        bbox = bbox || [Math.min(...xs), Math.min(...ys), Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs), Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys)];
        path = (c) => { c.beginPath(); region.forEach((q, i) => (i ? c.lineTo(q[0], q[1]) : c.moveTo(q[0], q[1]))); c.closePath(); };
      }
      if (!bbox) throw new Error('pen.hatch: a path function needs o.bbox');
      const [bx, by, bw, bh] = bbox;
      const ang = o.angle ?? -Math.PI / 4, gap = o.gap ?? 9;
      const ux = Math.cos(ang), uy = Math.sin(ang), vx = -uy, vy = ux;
      const cx = bx + bw / 2, cy = by + bh / 2, R = Math.hypot(bw, bh) / 2 + 4;
      const nLines = Math.ceil((2 * R) / gap);
      const sd0 = key(o);
      ctx.save();
      path(ctx);
      ctx.clip();
      const p = clamp(o.p ?? 1);
      for (let i = 0; i <= nLines; i++) {
        const lp = clamp(p * (nLines + 1) - i);
        if (lp <= 0) break;
        const d = -R + i * gap + shash(i, sd0) * gap * 0.12;
        const mx = cx + vx * d, my = cy + vy * d;
        drawStroke(ctx, [[mx - ux * R, my - uy * R], [mx + ux * R, my + uy * R]], { ...r, p: lp }, mixSeed(sd0, i));
      }
      ctx.restore();
      return pen;
    },
    /** Dots along a polyline every `gap` px (dotted leaders, trajectories). */
    dotted(pts, o = {}) {
      const r = res(o);
      const gap = o.gap ?? 11, rad = o.r ?? Math.max(1, r.width * 0.55);
      const P = resample(pts, 1.5);
      const sd = key(o);
      let acc = 0, next = 0;
      const total = plen(P), lim = clamp(o.p ?? 1) * total;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha *= r.alpha;
      ctx.fillStyle = r.color;
      let k = 0;
      for (let i = 1; i < P.length; i++) {
        acc += Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]);
        if (acc > lim) break;
        if (acc >= next) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(P[i][0] + shash(k, sd) * 0.8, P[i][1] + shash(k, sd + 1) * 0.8, rad * (0.85 + 0.3 * hash(k, sd + 2)), 0, TAU);
          ctx.fill();
          next += gap; k++;
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
      return pen;
    },
    /** Dashes along a polyline. */
    dashed(pts, o = {}) {
      const dash = o.dash ?? 14, gap = o.gap ?? 9;
      const P = resample(pts, 2);
      const total = plen(P), lim = clamp(o.p ?? 1) * total;
      const sd = key(o);
      let acc = 0, cur = [P[0]], on = true, mark = dash, idx = 0;
      for (let i = 1; i < P.length; i++) {
        acc += Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]);
        if (acc > lim) break;
        if (on) cur.push(P[i]);
        if (acc >= mark) {
          if (on) { drawStroke(ctx, cur, { ...res(o), jitter: 0.5, p: 1 }, mixSeed(sd, idx++)); }
          on = !on; mark += on ? dash : gap; cur = [P[i]];
        }
      }
      if (on && cur.length > 1) drawStroke(ctx, cur, { ...res(o), jitter: 0.5, p: 1 }, mixSeed(sd, idx));
      return pen;
    },
  };
  return pen;
}

// Baby Clawd: the Claude Code mascot as a baby. Clawd is a blocky clay body (10 units wide,
// 6 tall) on four stubby legs with two arm nubs and tall rounded-rectangle eyes; the baby is
// chubbier and nearly square, with rounder corners, shorter legs, bigger and lower eyes with
// a big highlight, a single curl on top and blush. Night: a sleep sack that wraps the legs
// (a soft bag with a zipper). Day: a onesie band across the lower body with two snaps.
// 'naked' is the bare clay body (after the bath), 'towel' adds a cape. A pacifier for the
// quiet and sleeping moments. One arm nub always stretches toward a bottom corner of the
// frame, holding the phone (we see the nub, never the phone).
//
// drawBaby(ctx, o): o.x, o.y face centre, o.r scale (design px; the body is 2.5r wide), o.C
// colours (skin = clay, skinShade, skinLight, ink, line, mouth, white, cloth, clothShade,
// towel, stain, blush, tear, paci), plus the pose fields in BABY_DEFAULTS. No state, no
// randomness: the same o always draws the same pixels.
//
// Opt-in options (off by default, so the 2am video draws exactly as before):
//   phone: false   no phone: the phone-side nub is a second free nub, posed by `other`
//                  (same modes as `free`), mirrored to the phoneArm side
//   cap: {...}     a white EEG cap over the top of the head with ink electrodes and a ribbon
//                  of wires trailing off (the baby-facts video, "the one in the little cap").
//                  cap = true or { to: [x, y] (wire end, in r around the face centre; far away
//                  = off-frame), sway: phase, wires: false, color, shade, electrode }

import { clamp, lerp, TAU } from '../renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix, rgba } from '../renderer/core/theme.js';

export const BABY_DEFAULTS = {
  r: 300, tilt: 0,
  outfit: 'onesie',          // 'onesie' | 'sack' | 'naked' | 'towel'
  eyes: 'open',              // 'open' | 'half' | 'closed' | 'squeeze' | 'wide' | 'focus'
  open: 1,                   // continuous eyelid 0..1 (overrides when < 1 for 'open'/'wide')
  lidSoft: false,            // heavy eyes without a lid line: the eye shortens from the top (drowsy, content)
  look: [0, 0],              // eye / highlight offset (-1..1)
  mouth: 'line',             // 'line' | 'o' | 'smile' | 'grin' | 'none' | 'press'
  mouthOpen: 1,
  brows: null,               // null | 'furrow' | 'up'  ('press' implies 'furrow')
  phoneArm: 'left',          // which bottom corner the phone nub reaches for
  free: { mode: 'down' },    // free nub: 'down' | 'wave' | 'up' | 'point' | 'hold' | 'none'
  stain: false, tears: 0, blush: 0,
  pacifier: 0,               // 0..1: the pacifier over the mouth (quiet and sleeping moments)
  flash: 0,                  // 0..1 hot-spot of the phone flash on the face
  phone: true,               // false: no phone, the phone-side nub is posed by `other`
  other: { mode: 'down' },   // the second free nub when phone is false
  cap: null,                 // EEG cap (see the header)
};

/** Geometry in units of r around the face centre (scenes place props and glows with it). */
export const BABY_GEOM = {
  body: { l: -1.25, r: 1.25, t: -1.0, b: 1.6, rad: 0.56, bulge: 0.05 },
  eye: { dx: 0.44, dy: 0.0, w: 0.31, h: 0.46, rad: 0.125 },
  mouthY: 0.47,
  cheek: { dx: 0.8, dy: 0.36 },
  arm: { x: 1.16, y: 0.46, t: 0.32 },
  legs: [-0.86, -0.38, 0.38, 0.86], legW: 0.3, legH: 0.3,
  sack: { t: 0.74, b: 2.08, w: 1.33 },
  band: 0.95,
};
const G0 = BABY_GEOM;

const ROSE = '#ee8a8a', SKY = '#8fd0f6';

/** The body outline: a rounded square whose sides bow out a little (chubby). */
function bodyPath(ctx, r) {
  const { l, r: rt, t, b, rad, bulge } = G0.body;
  const L = l * r, R = rt * r, T = t * r, B = b * r, k = rad * r, q = bulge * r;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(L + k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, T - q, R - k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, T, R, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R + q, (T + B) / 2, R, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, B, R - k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, B + q, L + k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, B, L, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L - q, (T + B) / 2, L, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, T, L + k, T);
  ctx.closePath();
}

function rr(ctx, x, y, w, h, k) {
  k = Math.max(0, Math.min(k, Math.abs(w) / 2, Math.abs(h) / 2));
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + k, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, k);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, k);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, k);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, k);
  ctx.closePath();
}

/**
 * A nub from `a` to `b` (thickness t0 at the root, t1 at the tip). The root end sits inside
 * the body and is not outlined, so the nub grows out of the body silhouette.
 */
function nub(ctx, a, b, t0, t1, fill, line, lw, { tipRound = 0.35, rootOpen = true } = {}) {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  const nx = -dy / d, ny = dx / d;
  const h0 = t0 / 2, h1 = t1 / 2, c = Math.min(h1 * 2 * tipRound, d * 0.4);
  const ux = dx / d, uy = dy / d;
  const p = (s, h, along = 0) => [a[0] + ux * s * d + nx * h + ux * along, a[1] + uy * s * d + ny * h + uy * along];
  const A = p(0, h0), Bq = p(1, h1, -c), Cq = p(1, -h1, -c), D = p(0, -h0);
  const T1 = p(1, h1), T2 = p(1, -h1);
  const outline = () => {
    ctx.moveTo(A[0], A[1]);
    ctx.lineTo(Bq[0], Bq[1]);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(T1[0], T1[1], (T1[0] + T2[0]) / 2, (T1[1] + T2[1]) / 2);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(T2[0], T2[1], Cq[0], Cq[1]);
    ctx.lineTo(D[0], D[1]);
  };
  ctx.beginPath(); outline(); ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
  if (line) {
    ctx.beginPath(); outline();
    if (!rootOpen) ctx.closePath();
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function eyeAt(ctx, k, o, C, r) {
  const E = G0.eye;
  let mode = o.eyes;
  const open = clamp(o.open);
  if ((mode === 'open' || mode === 'wide' || mode === 'half') && open < 0.18) mode = 'closed';
  const lx = o.look[0] * 0.07 * r, ly = o.look[1] * 0.06 * r;
  const ex = k * E.dx * r, ey = E.dy * r;
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.fillStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  if (mode === 'closed') {
    // sleeping: a soft downward arc
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.055 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.16 * r, ey + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, ey + 0.15 * r, ex + 0.16 * r, ey + 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
    return;
  }
  if (mode === 'squeeze') {
    // squeezed shut: > <
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.062 * r;
    const w = 0.13 * r, h = 0.135 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex + k * w, ey - h); ctx.lineTo(ex - k * w * 0.9, ey); ctx.lineTo(ex + k * w, ey + h); ctx.stroke();
    return;
  }
  if (mode === 'focus') {
    // concentration: a narrow flat slit
    rr(ctx, ex - 0.16 * r + lx * 0.5, ey + 0.02 * r, 0.32 * r, 0.12 * r, 0.05 * r); ctx.fill();

  const w = E.w * s * r, h = E.h * s * r;
  const x0 = ex - w / 2 + lx, y0 = ey - h / 2 + ly;
  const lid = mode === 'half' ? Math.min(open, 0.5) : open;

  // a heavy lid: its edge bows up a little and droops toward the outer corner (drowsy, not cross)

  if (o.lidSoft) {

    ctx.save();

    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) { const x = x0 - 0.1 * r + (w + 0.2 * r) * i / 8; ctx.lineTo(x, lidY(x)); }
    ctx.lineTo(x0 + w + 0.1 * r, y0 + 2 * h); ctx.lineTo(x0 - 0.1 * r, y0 + 2 * h); ctx.closePath(); ctx.clip();
  }
  rr(ctx, x0, y0, w, h, E.rad * s * r); ctx.fill();
  // the highlight: a big dot up and to the left, a small one below it
  const hx = x0 + w * 0.33 + o.look[0] * 0.02 * r, hy = Math.max(y0 + h * 0.27, lid < 1 ? lidY(x0 + w * 0.33) + w * 0.3 : -1e9) + o.look[1] * 0.02 * r;
  ctx.fillStyle = C.white || '#fff';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hx, hy, w * 0.26, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.7, y0 + h * 0.68, w * 0.11, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  if (mode === 'wide') { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.68, y0 + h * 0.3, w * 0.08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.restore();
  if (lid < 1) {
    // the eyelid's edge, sagging a little
    ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r;
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i <= 8; i++) { const x = x0 - 0.05 * r + (w + 0.1 * r) * i / 8; if (i) ctx.lineTo(x, lidY(x)); else ctx.moveTo(x, lidY(x)); }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function brow(ctx, k, kind, C, r) {
  const E = G0.eye;
  const ex = k * E.dx * r, by = (E.dy - E.h / 2 - 0.11) * r;
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.beginPath();
  if (kind === 'furrow') { ctx.moveTo(ex + k * 0.16 * r, by - 0.05 * r); ctx.lineTo(ex - k * 0.1 * r, by + 0.04 * r); }
  else { ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.12 * r, by + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, by - 0.07 * r, ex + 0.12 * r, by + 0.02 * r); }
  ctx.stroke();
}

function tears(ctx, phase, C, r) {
  const E = G0.eye;
  const col = C.tear || SKY;
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const x0 = k * (E.dx + 0.02) * r, y0 = (E.dy + E.h / 2 - 0.04) * r, y1 = 0.9 * r;
    const pts = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= 6; i++) {
      const f = i / 6;
      pts.push([x0 + k * 0.05 * r * f + Math.sin(phase * TAU + i * 1.3 + k) * 0.022 * r * f, lerp(y0, y1, f)]);
    }
    const stroke = (w, c) => {
      ctx.strokeStyle = c; ctx.lineWidth = w; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
      for (const p of pts.slice(1)) ctx.lineTo(p[0], p[1]);
      ctx.stroke();
    };
    stroke(0.13 * r, rgba(C.line, 0.6));
    stroke(0.1 * r, col);
    stroke(0.032 * r, rgba('#ffffff', 0.7));
    // a drop flicking off to the side
    for (const off of [0, 0.5]) {
      const ph = (phase * 1.6 + off + (k > 0 ? 0.25 : 0)) % 1;
      const dx = k * (0.22 + 0.5 * ph) * r, dy = (0.08 - 0.28 * Math.sin(Math.PI * ph) + 0.55 * ph * ph) * r;
      const s = (1 - 0.45 * ph) * r;
      ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(C.line, 0.5); ctx.lineWidth = 0.015 * r;
      const px = x0 + dx, py = y0 + dy, dr = 0.07 * s;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py - dr * 1.6); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(px + dr, py - dr * 0.2, px + dr * 0.9, py + dr * 0.25);
      ctx.arc(px, py + dr * 0.25, dr * 0.9, 0, Math.PI); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(px - dr, py - dr * 0.2, px, py - dr * 1.6); ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
}

function mouthAt(ctx, o, C, r) {
  const my = G0.mouthY * r;
  const mo = clamp(o.mouthOpen);
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.042 * r; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  ctx.fillStyle = C.mouth;
  switch (o.mouth) {
    case 'o': {
      const rx = (0.055 + 0.11 * mo) * r, ry = (0.04 + 0.16 * mo) * r, cy = my + 0.03 * r * mo;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      if (mo > 0.55) {
        ctx.save(); ctx.clip();
        ctx.fillStyle = mix(ROSE, C.mouth, 0.25);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy + ry * 0.75, rx * 0.7, ry * 0.42, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    }
    case 'smile':
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, my + 0.1 * r, 0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    case 'grin': {
      const d = (0.1 + 0.14 * mo) * r;
      const path = () => { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.bezierCurveTo(0.16 * r, my + d * 0.9, -0.16 * r, my + d * 0.9, -0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.closePath(); };
      path(); ctx.fill();
      ctx.save(); path(); ctx.clip();
      ctx.fillStyle = mix(ROSE, C.mouth, 0.2);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, my + d * 0.7, 0.11 * r, 0.07 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r; path(); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    }
    case 'press':
      // straining: a flat, slightly crimped line
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.11 * r, my); ctx.lineTo(-0.03 * r, my - 0.012 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.03 * r, my + 0.008 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.11 * r, my - 0.004 * r); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    case 'none':
      break;
    default:
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.08 * r, my); ctx.lineTo(0.08 * r, my); ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function pacifier(ctx, a, C, r) {
  const my = G0.mouthY * r + 0.02 * r;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(a);
  // the shield over the mouth, a button, and the ring hanging from it
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.032 * r; ctx.strokeStyle = C.line;
  ctx.fillStyle = C.paci || '#7fcbc0';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, my, 0.24 * r, 0.14 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.4);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-0.09 * r, my - 0.055 * r, 0.08 * r, 0.032 * r, -0.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  // the ring, hinged on a button in the middle of the shield
  const ry = my + 0.11 * r, rad = 0.11 * r;
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.085 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, ry, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.paciRing || '#f6efe2'; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, ry, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = mix(C.paci || '#7fcbc0', C.line, 0.3); ctx.strokeStyle = C.line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.02 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, my, 0.055 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

/** The EEG cap: a white cap hugging the top of the head, ink electrodes, wires trailing off. */
function eegCap(ctx, cap, C, r, lw) {
  const o = cap === true ? {} : cap;
  const white = o.color || '#fbfaf6', shade = o.shade || '#ddd7ca', line = C.line, inkc = o.electrode || C.ink;
  const capPath = () => {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-1.31 * r, -0.4 * r);
    ctx.lineTo(-1.32 * r, -0.6 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-1.33 * r, -1.06 * r, -0.78 * r, -1.08 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -1.22 * r, 0.78 * r, -1.08 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.33 * r, -1.06 * r, 1.32 * r, -0.6 * r);
    ctx.lineTo(1.31 * r, -0.4 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -0.52 * r, -1.31 * r, -0.4 * r);
    ctx.closePath();
  };
  ctx.save();
  capPath(); ctx.fillStyle = white; ctx.fill();
  ctx.save(); capPath(); ctx.clip();
  // a soft shade on the far side and along the rim band
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(shade, 0.7);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(1.15 * r, -0.62 * r, 0.42 * r, 0.5 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(shade, 1); ctx.lineWidth = 0.07 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-1.4 * r, -0.47 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -0.6 * r, 1.4 * r, -0.47 * r); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
  capPath(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.stroke();
  // the rim band's upper seam
  ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.55;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-1.3 * r, -0.53 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -0.66 * r, 1.3 * r, -0.53 * r); ctx.stroke();
  // electrodes: ink discs with a pale ring and a glint
  const E = [[-0.98, -0.7], [-0.5, -0.86], [0, -0.92], [0.5, -0.86], [0.98, -0.7], [-0.28, -1.05], [0.28, -1.05], [-0.8, -0.98], [0.8, -0.98]];
  const er = 0.075 * r;
  // wires first (under the electrodes): each lead runs to a hub above the cap, and from there
  // a narrow ribbon cable trails off toward `to` (far away = off-frame), sagging a little
  if (o.wires !== false) {
    const to = o.to || [1.7, -4.6];
    const sway = o.sway ?? 0;
    const hub = [0.42 * r, -1.38 * r];
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, lw * 0.32);
    E.forEach(([ex, ey]) => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(ex * r, ey * r);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex * r * 0.75 + hub[0] * 0.25, Math.min(ey * r, hub[1]) - 0.12 * r, hub[0], hub[1]);
      ctx.stroke();
    });
    const tx = to[0] * r, ty = to[1] * r;
    const d = Math.hypot(tx - hub[0], ty - hub[1]) || 1;
    const nx = -(ty - hub[1]) / d, ny = (tx - hub[0]) / d;
    const c1 = [hub[0] + (tx - hub[0]) * 0.3 + nx * (0.25 + 0.08 * Math.sin(sway)) * r, hub[1] + (ty - hub[1]) * 0.3 + ny * 0.25 * r + 0.35 * r];
    const c2 = [hub[0] + (tx - hub[0]) * 0.7 - nx * (0.2 + 0.08 * Math.sin(sway + 1.3)) * r, hub[1] + (ty - hub[1]) * 0.7 + 0.3 * r];
    for (const k of [-1, 0, 1]) {
      const off = k * 0.028 * r;
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, lw * (k ? 0.3 : 0.38));
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(hub[0] + nx * off * 0.3, hub[1] + ny * off * 0.3);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(c1[0] + nx * off, c1[1] + ny * off, c2[0] + nx * off, c2[1] + ny * off, tx + nx * off, ty + ny * off);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  for (const [ex, ey] of E) {
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(shade, 1);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r, ey * r, er * 1.45, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = inkc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r, ey * r, er, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.75);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r - er * 0.35, ey * r - er * 0.35, er * 0.28, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

/** A free nub on `side` (-1 left, +1 right): 'down' | 'wave' | 'up' | 'point' | 'hold' | 'none'. */
function freeNub(ctx, f, side, root, r, clay, line, lw) {
  const arm = G0.arm;
  const t = arm.t * r;
  if (f.mode === 'down') {
    nub(ctx, root(side), [side * 1.62 * r, 0.82 * r], t, t, clay, line, lw);
  } else if (f.mode === 'wave' || f.mode === 'up') {
    const a = f.angle ?? 0;
    const lift = f.mode === 'wave' ? 0.95 + 0.4 * Math.sin(a) : 1.2 + 0.25 * Math.sin(a);
    const len = 0.62 * r;
    nub(ctx, root(side), [side * (arm.x * r + Math.cos(lift) * len), arm.y * r - Math.sin(lift) * len], t, t * 1.05, clay, line, lw);
  } else if (f.mode === 'point') {
    // the nub coming at the lens: it grows, its flat end toward us
    const k = clamp(f.reach ?? 1);
    const hx = lerp(side * 1.5, side * 0.3, k) * r, hy = lerp(0.9, 1.0, k) * r, hr = lerp(0.2, 0.7, k) * r;
    nub(ctx, root(side), [hx, hy], t, hr * 1.6, clay, line, lw);
    rr(ctx, hx - hr, hy - hr * 0.9, 2 * hr, 1.8 * hr, hr * 0.45);
    ctx.fillStyle = mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.08); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
  } else if (f.mode === 'hold') {
    // the nub reaches the point the scene gives (head-relative units); the scene draws the prop
    const hx = (f.at?.[0] ?? side * 0.6) * r, hy = (f.at?.[1] ?? 1.2) * r;
    nub(ctx, root(side), [hx, hy], t, t * 1.15, clay, line, lw);
  }
}

export function drawBaby(ctx, opts) {
  const o = { ...BABY_DEFAULTS, ...opts, free: { ...BABY_DEFAULTS.free, ...(opts.free || {}) }, other: { ...BABY_DEFAULTS.other, ...(opts.other || {}) } };
  const { r, C } = o;
  const B = G0.body;
  const lw = r * 0.034;
  const s = o.phoneArm === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
  const fs = -s;
  const clay = o.blush > 0 ? mix(C.skin, '#e2574a', 0.1 * clamp(o.blush)) : C.skin;
  const clayDk = C.skinShade, clayLt = C.skinLight || mix(C.skin, '#ffffff', 0.35);
  const line = C.line, cloth = C.cloth, clothShade = C.clothShade;
  const arm = G0.arm;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(o.x, o.y);
  ctx.rotate(o.tilt);
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';

  // ---- behind the body: the towel cape, the curl, the legs ---------------------------------
  if (o.outfit === 'towel') {
    ctx.fillStyle = C.towel || '#f2f2f2';
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(line, 0.8); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.8;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-1.0 * r, -0.85 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-1.95 * r, 0.6 * r, -2.05 * r, 2.3 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-1.0 * r, 2.05 * r, 0, 2.35 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.0 * r, 2.05 * r, 2.05 * r, 2.3 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.95 * r, 0.6 * r, 1.0 * r, -0.85 * r);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(C.towel || '#f2f2f2', line, 0.3), 0.6); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.6;
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(k * 1.3 * r, 0.2 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(k * 1.62 * r, 1.2 * r, k * 1.6 * r, 2.1 * r); ctx.stroke(); }
  }
  // a single curl on top, sprouting from behind the top edge
  {
    const path = () => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-0.04 * r, -0.92 * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(-0.08 * r, -1.22 * r, 0.12 * r, -1.42 * r, 0.27 * r, -1.3 * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(0.38 * r, -1.2 * r, 0.27 * r, -1.06 * r, 0.16 * r, -1.13 * r);
    };
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    path(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.13 * r; ctx.stroke();
    path(); ctx.strokeStyle = clay; ctx.lineWidth = 0.13 * r - 2 * lw; ctx.stroke();
  }
  if (o.outfit !== 'sack') {
    ctx.fillStyle = clayDk; ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.9;
    for (const lx of G0.legs) {
      rr(ctx, (lx - G0.legW / 2) * r, (B.b - 0.12) * r, G0.legW * r, (G0.legH + 0.12) * r, 0.08 * r);
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }

  // ---- the body -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.fillStyle = clay;
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.save();
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.clip();
  // flat clay shading: a light patch up and left, a darker band low on the body
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clayLt, 0.42);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-0.55 * r, -0.6 * r, 0.52 * r, 0.22 * r, -0.12, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  if (o.outfit === 'naked' || o.outfit === 'towel') {
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clayDk, 0.55);
    ctx.fillRect(-2 * r, 1.08 * r, 4 * r, 2 * r);
  }
  if (o.flash > 0) {
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(-0.1 * r, -0.05 * r, 0, -0.1 * r, -0.05 * r, 1.25 * r);
    g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffffff', 0.5 * o.flash));
    g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffffff', 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(-2 * r, -2 * r, 4 * r, 4 * r);
  }
  if (o.outfit === 'onesie') {
    // the onesie: a cloth band across the lower body, two snaps
    const y = G0.band * r;
    ctx.fillStyle = cloth;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, y - 0.05 * r, 2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.lineTo(2 * r, 3 * r); ctx.lineTo(-2 * r, 3 * r); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clothShade, 0.7);
    ctx.fillRect(-2 * r, (B.b - 0.13) * r, 4 * r, r);
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.9;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, y - 0.05 * r, 2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
      ctx.fillStyle = mix(cloth, '#9aa3ad', 0.45);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(k * 0.2 * r, (B.b - 0.24) * r, 0.055 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.55; ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.8);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(k * 0.2 * r - 0.015 * r, (B.b - 0.255) * r, 0.018 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  if (o.stain) {
    const st = C.stain || '#e9dfc8';
    const blobs = [[-0.42, 0.98, 0.17], [-0.12, 1.12, 0.12], [0.24, 0.96, 0.1], [-0.25, 1.33, 0.14], [0.5, 1.18, 0.08], [0.05, 0.82, 0.07]];
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(st, 0.95);
    for (const [sx, sy, sr] of blobs) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(sx * r, sy * r, sr * r * 1.25, sr * r, 0.4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(st, line, 0.35), 0.55); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.55;
    for (const [sx, sy, sr] of blobs.slice(0, 4)) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(sx * r, sy * r, sr * r * 1.25, sr * r, 0.4, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); }
    // a drip running down from the mouth
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(st, 0.95);
    rr(ctx, 0.03 * r, (G0.mouthY + 0.04) * r, 0.06 * r, 0.18 * r, 0.03 * r); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0.06 * r, (G0.mouthY + 0.23) * r, 0.05 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw;
  ctx.stroke();

  // ---- the sleep sack: a soft bag over the lower body and the legs, zipped up the middle ---
  if (o.outfit === 'sack') {
    const S = G0.sack;
    const sackPath = () => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-S.w * r * 0.97, S.t * r);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, (S.t - 0.07) * r, S.w * r * 0.97, S.t * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo((S.w + 0.12) * r, (S.t + 0.5) * r, (S.w + 0.1) * r, (S.b - 0.35) * r, (S.w - 0.25) * r, S.b * r);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, (S.b + 0.08) * r, -(S.w - 0.25) * r, S.b * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(-(S.w + 0.1) * r, (S.b - 0.35) * r, -(S.w + 0.12) * r, (S.t + 0.5) * r, -S.w * r * 0.97, S.t * r);
      ctx.closePath();
    };
    sackPath(); ctx.fillStyle = cloth; ctx.fill();
    ctx.save(); sackPath(); ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clothShade, 0.75);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(1.55 * r, 1.5 * r, 0.5 * r, 1.2 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    // the binding along the top hem
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(clothShade, 1); ctx.lineWidth = 0.09 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-S.w * r, (S.t + 0.04) * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, (S.t - 0.03) * r, S.w * r, (S.t + 0.04) * r); ctx.stroke();
    // zipper: a seam with teeth and the pull tab at the top
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(clothShade, line, 0.35), 0.95); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.75;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0.06 * r, (S.t + 0.06) * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0.1 * r, (S.t + 0.7) * r, 0.04 * r, (S.b + 0.1) * r); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.45;
    for (let i = 1; i < 11; i++) {
      const f = i / 11, zy = lerp(S.t + 0.08, S.b, f) * r, zx = (0.06 + 0.04 * Math.sin(Math.PI * f)) * r;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(zx - 0.03 * r, zy); ctx.lineTo(zx + 0.03 * r, zy); ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    sackPath(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = mix(clothShade, '#ffffff', 0.25);
    rr(ctx, 0.0, (S.t + 0.06) * r, 0.12 * r, 0.2 * r, 0.04 * r); ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.6; ctx.stroke();
  }

  // ---- the EEG cap (opt-in) ---------------------------------------------------------------------
  if (o.cap) eegCap(ctx, o.cap, C, r, lw);

  // ---- the phone nub: stretched to the bottom corner, the phone out of frame -------------------
  const root = (k) => [k * arm.x * r, arm.y * r];
  if (o.phone !== false) nub(ctx, root(s), [s * 2.0 * r, 3.6 * r], arm.t * 1.05 * r, arm.t * 2.1 * r, clay, line, lw);
  else freeNub(ctx, o.other, s, root, r, clay, line, lw);

  // ---- the face -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // blush: always a little (a baby), more on demand
  const bl = 0.32 + 0.68 * clamp(o.blush);
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const bx = k * G0.cheek.dx * r, by = G0.cheek.dy * r;
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(C.blush || ROSE, 0.55 * bl);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx, by, 0.17 * r, 0.09 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    if (o.blush > 0.6) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(C.blush || ROSE, line, 0.35), 0.8); ctx.lineWidth = 0.022 * r;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx + (i - 1.4) * 0.075 * r, by + 0.045 * r); ctx.lineTo(bx + (i - 0.9) * 0.075 * r, by - 0.045 * r); ctx.stroke(); }
    }
  }
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) eyeAt(ctx, k, o, C, r);
  const brows = o.brows || (o.mouth === 'press' ? 'furrow' : null);
  if (brows) for (const k of [-1, 1]) brow(ctx, k, brows, C, r);
  if (o.tears > 0) tears(ctx, o.tears, C, r);
  if (!(o.pacifier >= 0.5)) mouthAt(ctx, o, C, r);
  if (o.pacifier > 0) pacifier(ctx, o.pacifier, C, r);

  // ---- the free nub ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  freeNub(ctx, o.free, fs, root, r, clay, line, lw);
  ctx.restore();
}

export function babyColors(G, outfit = 'onesie') {

    towel: G.towel, stain: G.stain, blush: G.blush, tear: G.tear, paci: G.paci, paciRing: G.paciRing,
  };
}

// Adult Clawd: the Claude Code mascot at full size (the parent in the 2am video, the
// scientists and parents of the baby-facts video). Moved here from
// songs/2am-crying/video/props.js, which re-exports it unchanged.

import { TAU, clamp } from '../renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix, rgba } from '../renderer/core/theme.js';
import { rrect } from '../renderer/core/text.js';
import { makePen } from '../renderer/core/ink.js';

/**
 * Adult Clawd (the parent): the classic proportions, a 10u x 6u clay block on four legs with
 * two arm nubs and tall slit eyes. (x, y) = the ground point between the feet; u = unit.
 *   pose 'stand' | 'sit' (on the floor, legs out toward us) | 'slump' (in a chair, tipped back)
 *   eyes 'open' | 'tired' (heavy lids) | 'closed' | null (a pure silhouette)
 *   mouth 'o' (asleep, breathing: breath 0..1) | 'line' | null
 * color fills everything when there is no `clay` (a night silhouette); rim = a rim-light colour.
 * Close-ups (the bridge, held in the parent's arms): h = body height in u (default 6; the face
 * keeps its place under the top edge), arms = null leaves the side nubs off, shine adds
 * highlights to 'open' eyes and look = [x, y] (-1..1) shifts them, shoulder = { k, q, at } (in u)
 * rounds the top corners (radius k) and flares the sides out by q over `at` below them: the
 * body seen from a phone held low, a rounded silhouette instead of a slab.
 *
 * style: 'ink' (opt-in; default is the clay drawing above) draws Clawd as ink line art on
 * paper: a hand-wobbled outline (shared/renderer/core/ink.js), the body and nubs filled with
 * `paper` (so it occludes what is behind it), hatching instead of the darker clay (the legs
 * and the low band; hatch: false leaves it off), eyes and mouth in ink. Extra options: pen (a
 * makePen() pen; default a pen seeded 'adult-clawd' in `ink`), paper ('#f4efe3'), width (the
 * outline in design px, default 0.17u clamped to 1.6..3.4), p (0..1 draw-in: the outline grows, the
 * paper inside fades in with it, the face appears from 0.6). Supports
 * pose 'stand' | 'sit', eyes, mouth, look, lean, arms; not shoulder or rim.
 */
export function adultClawd(ctx, opts) {
  if (opts.style === 'ink') return inkAdult(ctx, opts);
  return clayAdult(ctx, opts);
}

function clayAdult(ctx, { x, y, u, pose = 'stand', color = '#14151b', clay = null, clayDk = null, ink = '#1e1412', rim = null, eyes = null, eyeColor = null, lean = 0, mouth = null, breath = 0.5, arms = [0.25, 0.25], h = 6, look = null, shine = null, shoulder = null }) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(lean);
  const fill = clay || color, dk = clayDk || (clay ? mix(clay, '#000000', 0.25) : color);
  const lw = clay ? Math.max(2, u * 0.16) : 0;
  const stroke = () => { if (lw) { ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.stroke(); } };
  const legH = pose === 'stand' ? 2.2 : 0;
  const top = -(h + legH) * u, bot = -legH * u;
  // arm nubs (behind the body), raise angles in radians
  if (arms) for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const a = arms[k < 0 ? 0 : 1];
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(k * 4.9 * u, top + 3.5 * u);
    ctx.rotate(k < 0 ? a : -a);
    rrect(ctx, k < 0 ? -2.2 * u : 0, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u);
    ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // legs: standing under the body; sitting, the feet point at us below the front edge
  ctx.fillStyle = dk;
  for (const lx of [-4, -2, 1, 3]) {
    if (pose === 'stand') { rrect(ctx, lx * u, -2.4 * u, u, 2.4 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill(); stroke(); }
    else { rrect(ctx, (lx - 0.1) * u, bot - 0.5 * u, 1.2 * u, 1.1 * u, 0.3 * u); ctx.fill(); stroke(); }
  }
  // body
  const body = () => {
    if (!shoulder) { rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, h * u, 0.3 * u); return; }
    const k = shoulder.k * u, q = shoulder.q * u, a = shoulder.at * u, c = 0.3 * u;
    const L = -5 * u, R = 5 * u, T = top, B = top + h * u;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(L + k, T); ctx.lineTo(R - k, T);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, T, R, T + k);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(R, T + k + a * 0.45, R + q, T + k + a * 0.55, R + q, T + k + a);
    ctx.lineTo(R + q, B - c); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R + q, B, R + q - c, B);
    ctx.lineTo(L - q + c, B); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L - q, B, L - q, B - c);
    ctx.lineTo(L - q, T + k + a);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(L - q, T + k + a * 0.55, L, T + k + a * 0.45, L, T + k);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, T, L + k, T);
    ctx.closePath();
  };
  body();
  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
  if (clay) {
    ctx.save(); body(); ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(dk, 0.5); ctx.fillRect(-5 * u, top + (h === 6 ? 4.2 : h - 1.8) * u, 10 * u, 2 * u);
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.35), 0.35);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-1.6 * u, top + 1.6 * u, 3.4 * u, 1.3 * u, -0.08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
    body(); stroke();
  }
  if (rim) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = rim; ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.3; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-4.6 * u, top + 0.1 * u); ctx.lineTo(4.7 * u, top + 0.1 * u); ctx.lineTo(4.95 * u, top + 2.5 * u); ctx.stroke();
  }
  adultFace(ctx, { u, top, ink, eyeColor, eyes, look, shine, mouth, breath, clay, lw });
  ctx.restore();
}

/** Adult Clawd's eyes and mouth (shared by the clay and the ink styles). */
function adultFace(ctx, { u, top, ink, eyeColor, eyes, look, shine, mouth, breath, clay, lw }) {
  // face: eyes at (+-2.5u, 2u below the top), mouth 3.7u below the top
  const ec = eyeColor || ink;
  if (eyes) {
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
      const ex = k * 2.5 * u, ey = top + 2 * u;
      ctx.fillStyle = ec; ctx.strokeStyle = ec; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      if (eyes === 'closed') {
        ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.32;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.8 * u, ey + 0.2 * u); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, ey + 0.8 * u, ex + 0.8 * u, ey + 0.2 * u); ctx.stroke();
      } else if (eyes === 'tired') {
        rrect(ctx, ex - 0.5 * u, ey + 0.05 * u, u, 0.95 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
        ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.24;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.85 * u, ey); ctx.lineTo(ex + 0.85 * u, ey + k * 0.12 * u); ctx.stroke();
      } else if (shine || look) {
        // open, with highlights: the eye and its shine follow the look
        const lx = (look ? look[0] : 0) * 0.22 * u, ly = (look ? look[1] : 0) * 0.3 * u;
        const x0 = ex - 0.5 * u + lx, y0 = ey - u + ly;
        rrect(ctx, x0, y0, u, 2 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
        if (shine) {
          ctx.fillStyle = shine;
          // the shine follows the look (looking down: the big one sinks toward the middle)
          const sx = (look ? look[0] : 0) * 0.14 * u, sy = (look ? look[1] : 0) * 0.42 * u;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + 0.34 * u + sx, y0 + 0.52 * u + sy, 0.25 * u, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + 0.68 * u + sx * 0.5, y0 + 1.42 * u + sy * 0.3, 0.1 * u, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        }
      } else {
        rrect(ctx, ex - 0.5 * u, ey - u, u, 2 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  }
  if (mouth === 'o') {
    const o = 0.75 + 0.25 * breath;
    ctx.fillStyle = clay ? '#4a1f2a' : rgba('#000000', 0.55);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, top + 3.9 * u, 0.6 * u, 0.85 * u * o, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    if (lw) { ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.8; ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.stroke(); }
  } else if (mouth === 'line') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = ec; ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.25;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.7 * u, top + 3.8 * u); ctx.lineTo(0.7 * u, top + 3.8 * u); ctx.stroke();
  }
}

/** Adult Clawd as ink line art on paper (style: 'ink'). */
function inkAdult(ctx, { x, y, u, pose = 'stand', ink = '#1d1a17', paper = '#f4efe3', pen = null, width = null, hatch = true, p = 1,
  eyes = 'open', eyeColor = null, lean = 0, mouth = null, breath = 0.5, arms = [0.25, 0.25], h = 6, look = null, shine = null }) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(lean);
  const lw = width ?? clamp(u * 0.17, 1.6, 3.4);
  const P = (pen || makePen(ctx, 'adult-clawd', { color: ink })).with({ color: ink, width: lw, wobble: Math.min(2.6, u * 0.22) });
  const H = P.with({ width: Math.max(1, lw * 0.42), wobble: 0.6 });
  const legH = pose === 'stand' ? 2.2 : 0;
  const top = -(h + legH) * u, bot = -legH * u;
  // the paper inside arrives with the outline (it hides what is behind once the shape is drawn)
  const fillA = clamp(p * 1.6);
  const fillR = (x0, y0, w0, h0, r0) => { if (fillA <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= fillA; rrect(ctx, x0, y0, w0, h0, r0); ctx.fillStyle = paper; ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); };
  // arm nubs, behind the body
  if (arms) for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const a = arms[k < 0 ? 0 : 1];
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(k * 4.9 * u, top + 3.5 * u);
    ctx.rotate(k < 0 ? a : -a);
    const ax = k < 0 ? -2.2 * u : 0;
    fillR(ax, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u);
    P.rrect(ax, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u, { p, k: `arm${k}` });
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // legs: hatched (the darker clay)
  for (const lx of [-4, -2, 1, 3]) {
    const r0 = pose === 'stand' ? [lx * u, -2.4 * u, u, 2.4 * u] : [(lx - 0.1) * u, bot - 0.5 * u, 1.2 * u, 1.1 * u];
    fillR(r0[0], r0[1], r0[2], r0[3], 0.15 * u);
    if (hatch) H.hatch(r0, { gap: Math.max(4, u * 0.38), p, k: `leg${lx}` });
    P.rect(r0[0], r0[1], r0[2], r0[3], { p, over: Math.min(3, u * 0.2), k: `legr${lx}` });
  }
  // body: paper, a hatched band low on it, the outline
  fillR(-5 * u, top, 10 * u, h * u, 0.3 * u);
  if (hatch) H.hatch([-5 * u, top + (h === 6 ? 4.2 : h - 1.8) * u, 10 * u, 1.8 * u], { gap: Math.max(4, u * 0.42), angle: -0.9, p, k: 'band' });
  P.rect(-5 * u, top, 10 * u, h * u, { p, over: Math.min(4, u * 0.3), k: 'body' });
  if (p >= 0.6) {
    ctx.globalAlpha *= Math.min(1, (p - 0.6) / 0.25);
    adultFace(ctx, { u, top, ink, eyeColor: eyeColor || ink, eyes, look, shine, mouth, breath, clay: null, lw: lw * 0.8 });
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// The chorus (and the final chorus): the apparatus bench, then the replication.
//
// Bench: a long ink bench with the stations named in the lyric, left to right: scanner ring,
// slope, EEG cap on a stand, swab, a ruler marked 7 and 10 (months). The baby rides a cart
// and reaches each station on its word ("scanner", "slope", "cap" (the cap drops on),
// "swab" (it taps the cheek), "seven, then at ten" (the ruler ticks)). A station whose word
// the take does not sing (chorus 2 sings only the first line) is reached at an interpolated
// time. On "subject", "sample" and "n of one" the baby gets labelled.
//
// Replication: on "Will it replicate?" the bench erases, the baby moves to the centre and
// divides like a cell on each "replicate" (one, two, four, eight, sixteen), each copy an ink
// outline, the original orange, into a 4x4 grid. Final chorus: all four divisions on the
// first "Will it replicate?" (half a beat apart), then a row turns orange on each "(I did.)",
// on "One year" and on "Ask me in a year": all sixteen orange, the page lighting up.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, babyColors, blink } from '../kit.js';
import { isReplicateLine } from '../labs.js';

const BENCH = { top: 612, x0: 170, x1: 1750, legs: [262, 1658], floor: 806 };
const ST = { scanner: 470, slope: 790, cap: 1080, swab: 1350, ruler: 1600 };
const CART = { w: 196, h: 30, wheel: 17, start: 262 };
const BR = 58;                      // the baby on the cart
const GRID = { cx: 860, cy: 470, dx: 300, dy: 172, r: [92, 74, 60, 50, 43] };

// ---- timing ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Everything time-like for one chorus window, from its lines (pure; cheap enough per frame). */
function plan(C) {
  const lines = C.lines;
  const ri = lines.findIndex(isReplicateLine);
  const bench = ri < 0 ? lines : lines.slice(0, ri), repl = ri < 0 ? [] : lines.slice(ri);
  const benchStart = C.start, replStart = repl.length ? repl[0].start - 0.2 : Infinity;
  const benchEnd = Math.min(replStart, C.end);
  const inBench = (m) => { for (let k = 0; k < bench.length; k++) { const w = C.word(m, { line: lines.indexOf(bench[k]) }); if (w) return w.start; } return null; };
  // station arrival times: on the word, else interpolated
  const names = ['scanner', 'slope', 'cap', 'swab', 'seven', 'ten'];
  const tw = names.map((n) => inBench(n));
  const first = bench[0] ? bench[0].start : benchStart;
  const lo = Math.min(benchEnd - 0.5, (bench[0] ? first + Math.min(bench[0].end - first, 2.4) * 0.35 : benchStart + 0.6));
  const hi = benchEnd - 0.35;
  const known = tw.map((x, i) => (x != null ? [i, x] : null)).filter(Boolean);
  const times = tw.map((x, i) => {
    if (x != null) return x;
    const a = [...known].reverse().find(([j]) => j < i), b = known.find(([j]) => j > i);
    const ia = a ? a[0] : -1, ta = a ? a[1] : lo, ib = b ? b[0] : names.length, tb = b ? b[1] : hi;
    return lerp(ta, tb, (i - ia) / (ib - ia));
  });
  // labels on the first line
  const l0 = bench[0] ? lines.indexOf(bench[0]) : -1;
  const lab = (m) => (l0 >= 0 ? C.word(m, { line: l0 }) : null);
  const labels = [['subject', lab('subject')], ['sample', lab('sample')], ['n = 1', lab('one')]];
  // divisions
  const reps = [];
  for (const l of repl) for (const w of l.words) if (/^\(?replicate/i.test(w.w)) reps.push(w.start);
  let div;
  if (C.plate === 'final' || reps.length < 4) {
    const t1 = reps.length ? reps[0] : (isFinite(replStart) ? replStart + 0.6 : Infinity);
    div = [0, 1, 2, 3].map((k) => t1 + (k * C.B.beatDur) / 2);
  } else div = reps.slice(0, 4);
  // final: the rows turn orange
  let rows = null;
  if (C.plate === 'final') {
    const did = [];
    // the start of each "(I did.)": its first backing word
    for (const l of repl) { const b = l.words.find((w) => w.backing); if (b && /did/i.test(l.words.filter((w) => w.backing).map((w) => w.w).join(' '))) did.push(b.start); }
    const yearL = repl.find((l) => /^fifteen babies/.test(l.n)), askL = repl.find((l) => /ask me in a year/.test(l.n));
    const pick = (l, m) => (l ? (l.words.find((w) => C.L.norm(w.w) === m)?.start ?? l.start) : null);
    const trig = [did[0] ?? null, did[1] ?? null, pick(yearL, 'year'), pick(askL, 'ask')];
    const a = div[3] + 0.8, b = C.end - 1.2;
    rows = trig.map((x, i) => (x != null ? x : lerp(a, b, i / 3)));
    for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) rows[i] = Math.max(rows[i], rows[i - 1] + 0.2);
  }
  return { bench, repl, benchStart, benchEnd, replStart, times, labels, div, rows };
}

// cart x at t: arrives at each station on its time, dwells, moves on
function cartX(t, T) {
  const xs = [ST.scanner, ST.slope, ST.cap, ST.swab, ST.ruler, ST.ruler];
  let x = CART.start, prevT = T.times[0] - Math.min(0.9, Math.max(0.3, T.times[0] - T.benchStart - 0.2));
  let prevX = CART.start;
  for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
    const ti = T.times[i];
    if (t >= ti) { x = xs[i]; prevX = xs[i]; prevT = ti; continue; }
    const leave = i === 0 ? prevT : Math.min(prevT + 0.22, ti - 0.12);
    return lerp(prevX, xs[i], ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, leave, ti)));
  }
  return x;
}

const rampY = (x) => { const d = Math.abs(x - ST.slope); return d < 92 ? -70 * (1 - d / 92) : 0; };

// ---- the stations ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

function drawBench(ctx, C, p, alpha) {
  if (alpha <= 0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  const pen = C.pen('bench', { width: 2.6 });
  const thin = pen.with({ width: 1.5, color: P.ink2 });
  const b = BENCH;
  pen.line(b.x0, b.top, b.x1, b.top, { p: seg(p, 0, 0.5), k: 'top' });
  pen.line(b.x0 + 6, b.top + 20, b.x1 - 6, b.top + 20, { p: seg(p, 0.05, 0.55), k: 'top2' });
  pen.line(b.x0, b.top, b.x0 + 6, b.top + 20, { p: seg(p, 0.05, 0.2), k: 'endL' });
  pen.line(b.x1, b.top, b.x1 - 6, b.top + 20, { p: seg(p, 0.4, 0.55), k: 'endR' });
  for (const lx of b.legs) {
    pen.line(lx - 40, b.top + 20, lx - 58, b.floor, { p: seg(p, 0.2, 0.6), k: `legA${lx}` });
    pen.line(lx + 40, b.top + 20, lx + 58, b.floor, { p: seg(p, 0.25, 0.65), k: `legB${lx}` });
    thin.line(lx - 50, b.top + 110, lx + 50, b.top + 110, { p: seg(p, 0.4, 0.7), k: `brace${lx}` });
  }
  thin.line(b.x0 - 40, b.floor, b.x1 + 40, b.floor, { p: seg(p, 0.3, 0.9), k: 'floor' });
  // station labels, under the bench top
  const L = [['scanner', ST.scanner], ['slope', ST.slope], ['EEG cap', ST.cap], ['swab', ST.swab], ['ruler (mo)', ST.ruler]];
  L.forEach(([s, x], i) => text(ctx, s, x, b.top + 60, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.45 + i * 0.08, 0.7 + i * 0.08) }));
  ctx.restore();
}

function ringPts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1) { const out = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 40; i++) { const a = lerp(a0, a1, i / 40); out.push([cx + Math.cos(a) * rx, cy + Math.sin(a) * ry]); } return out; }

/** The scanner ring: the back half (behind the cart) or the front half. */
function drawScanner(ctx, C, p, half) {
  const cx = ST.scanner, cy = BENCH.top - 168;
  const pen = C.pen('scanner', { width: 2.6 });
  const [a0, a1] = half === 'back' ? [Math.PI / 2, (3 * Math.PI) / 2] : [-Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 2];
  const outer = ringPts(cx, cy, 64, 168, a0, a1), inner = ringPts(cx, cy, 40, 140, a0, a1);
  if (half === 'front') pen.hatch([...outer, ...inner.slice().reverse()], { gap: 8, p, angle: -1.1, k: 'hatch' });
  pen.stroke(outer, { p, k: `o-${half}` });
  pen.stroke(inner, { p, k: `i-${half}` });
  if (half === 'back') {
    // the pedestal
    pen.poly([[cx - 52, BENCH.top], [cx - 30, cy + 150], [cx + 30, cy + 150], [cx + 52, BENCH.top]], { p, k: 'ped' });
  }
}

function drawSlope(ctx, C, p) {
  const pen = C.pen('slope', { width: 2.6 });
  const x = ST.slope, y = BENCH.top;
  const tri = [[x - 92, y], [x, y - 70], [x + 92, y]];
  pen.hatch(tri, { gap: 9, p, k: 'hatch' });
  pen.poly(tri, { p, k: 'ramp' });
}

/** The cap stand; the cap hangs from it until it drops (drop = 0..1). */
function drawCapStand(ctx, C, p, drop, headY) {
  const pen = C.pen('capstand', { width: 2.4 });
  const px = ST.cap + 110, topY = 232;
  pen.line(px, BENCH.top, px, topY, { p, k: 'post' });
  pen.line(px - 30, BENCH.top, px + 30, BENCH.top, { p, k: 'foot' });
  pen.line(px, topY, ST.cap, topY, { p, k: 'arm' });
  if (drop >= 1) { pen.with({ width: 1.3 }).line(ST.cap, topY, ST.cap, topY + 30, { p, k: 'hook' }); return; }
  const hang = topY + 92;
  const capY = lerp(hang, headY, ease.inQuad(drop));
  pen.with({ width: 1.3 }).line(ST.cap, topY, ST.cap, capY - 1.15 * BR, { p, k: 'string' });
  drawLooseCap(ctx, C, ST.cap, capY, BR, p);
}

/** An empty EEG cap in ink (paper inside, electrodes), shaped like the baby's (BR units). */
function drawLooseCap(ctx, C, x, y, r, p) {
  const pen = C.pen('loosecap', { width: 2.2 });
  const pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 24; i++) {
    const a = Math.PI + (i / 24) * Math.PI;
    pts.push([x + Math.cos(a) * 1.3 * r, y - 0.42 * r + Math.sin(a) * 0.75 * r]);
  }
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath(); pts.forEach((q, i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(q[0], q[1]) : ctx.moveTo(q[0], q[1]))); ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = P.cap; ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  pen.stroke([...pts, pts[0]], { p, k: 'cap' });
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  for (const [ex, ey] of [[-0.85, -0.75], [-0.4, -1.0], [0, -1.08], [0.4, -1.0], [0.85, -0.75]]) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = p;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + ex * r, y + ey * r, 0.075 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

/** The swab on its arm; tap = 0..1..0 moves the bud to the cheek at (cx, cy). */
function drawSwab(ctx, C, p, tap, cheek) {
  const pen = C.pen('swab', { width: 2.4 });
  const px = ST.swab + 128, topY = 300;
  pen.line(px, BENCH.top, px, topY, { p, k: 'post' });
  pen.line(px - 28, BENCH.top, px + 28, BENCH.top, { p, k: 'foot' });
  const rest = [ST.swab + 92, 420];

  pen.with({ width: 3.4 }).line(handle[0], handle[1], bud[0], bud[1], { p, k: 'stick' });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(bud[0], bud[1]);
  ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(handle[1] - bud[1], handle[0] - bud[0]));
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-4, 0, 15, 10, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  pen.with({ width: 2 }).ellipse(bud[0] - 3, bud[1] + 2, 14, 10, { p, k: 'bud', rot: 0.7 });
}

/** The ruler: ticks, 7 and 10 marked; marks = [k7, k10] 0..1 as each is measured. */
function drawRuler(ctx, C, p, marks, heads) {
  const pen = C.pen('ruler', { width: 2.4 });

async function main() {
  const audioJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(abs(songDir), dataDir || 'data', 'audio.json'), 'utf8'));
  const server = createServer(REPO);
  const port = await listen(server, 0);
  // output size from the layout's design size unless given
  let w = Number(opt('w', 0)), h = Number(opt('h', 0));
  if (!w || !h) {
    const portrait = (layout || '9x16') === '9x16';
    w = w || (portrait ? 540 : 960); h = h || (portrait ? 960 : 540);
  }
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const nW = outMp4 ? workersN : 1;
  // launch all workers; if any fails to start, close the ones that did (no orphaned Chromes)
  const launched = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: nW }, (_, i) => launchWorker(port, w, h, i)));
  const bad = launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'rejected');
  if (bad.length) {
    await Promise.all(launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled').map((x) => closeWorker(x.value)));
    server.closeAllConnections?.(); server.close();
    throw bad[0].reason;
  }
  const workers = launched.map((x) => x.value);
  const info = workers[0].info;
  log(`${workers.length} worker(s) ready in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s · ${info.w}x${info.h} @${fps} · layout ${info.layout} · song ${info.duration.toFixed(2)}s · ${info.windows.length} windows`);
  const P = workers[0].page;
  reportSections(info);

  try {
    if (stills) {
      const dir = abs(opt('stills-dir', path.join(songDir, 'renders', 'stills')));
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      for (const s of stills.split(',').map(Number)) {
        const url = await P.evaluate((t) => window.__render.png(t), s);
        const f = path.join(dir, `still-${info.layout}-${s.toFixed(3)}.png`);
        fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
        log('still', path.relative(REPO, f));
      }
    }

    if (sheetOut) {
      const mode = opt('sheet-mode', 'clips');
      let cells;
      if (mode === 'even') {
        const n = Number(opt('count', 24));
        cells = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ t: (info.duration * (i + 0.5)) / n, label: `#${i + 1}` }));
      } else cells = await P.evaluate(() => window.__render.heroCells());
      const portrait = info.h > info.w;
      const cols = Number(opt('cols', portrait ? 10 : 5));
      const cellW = Number(opt('cell-w', portrait ? 216 : 384));
      const title = opt('title', `${songDir} · ${info.layout} · ${cells.length} cells · ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16)}`);
      const url = await P.evaluate((c, o) => window.__render.sheet(c, o), cells, { cols, cellW, title });
      const f = abs(sheetOut);
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
      log(`sheet (${cells.length} cells) -> ${path.relative(REPO, f)}`);
    }

    if (check) await runChecks(workers, info);

    if (outMp4) await encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson);
  } finally {
    await Promise.all(workers.map(closeWorker));
    server.closeAllConnections?.();
    server.close();
  }
}

/** Where line sections came from, and which section-derived plates exist (loud when missing). */
function reportSections(info) {
  const S = info.sections || {};
  const fmt = (t) => `${Math.floor(t / 60)}:${(t % 60).toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')}`;
  log(`line sections from ${S.source || '?'}: ${(S.list || []).join(', ')}`);
  for (const sp of S.expect || []) {
    const ws = info.windows.filter((x) => x.fromSection === sp.label);
    if (ws.length) log(`section plate ok: ${sp.label} -> ${ws.map((x) => `${x.plate}${x.params.mode ? ':' + x.params.mode : ''} ${fmt(x.start)}-${fmt(x.end)} (${(x.end - x.start).toFixed(1)}s)`).join(', ')}`);
    else log(`section plate MISSING: ${sp.label} (no lyric line in section "${sp.section}")`);
  }
}

// Smoke test for the preview page: loads index.html in headless Chrome with each given
// query, waits for the preview to start, reports errors and saves a screenshot.
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --q "t=23" --q "layout=16x9&t=40" [--shots dir]

import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, defaultViewport: { width: 1280, height: 1000 } });
let bad = 0;
for (const q of qs.length ? qs : ['t=0']) {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  const errs = [];
  p.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(String(e)));
  p.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/favicon|404/.test(m.text())) errs.push(m.text()); });
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${song}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await p.waitForFunction('window.__preview || (window.__render && window.__render.error)', { timeout: 30000 });
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
  const info = await p.evaluate(() => ({ err: document.getElementById('pv-err')?.textContent || '', bar: [...document.querySelectorAll('.pv-bar span')].map((s) => s.textContent).join(' | ') }));
  if (info.err || errs.length) bad++;
  console.log(`[smoke] ${q}: ${info.bar}${info.err ? '\n  RENDER ERROR: ' + info.err : ''}${errs.length ? '\n  ' + errs.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  if (shots) { fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true }); await p.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, `preview-${q.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`) }); }
  await p.close();
}
await b.close();
srv.close();
process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);

// Math, easing, envelopes and seeded hashing. Everything here is pure: a frame is a
// function of song time, so nothing may carry state between calls.

export const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
export const clamp = (x, a = 0, b = 1) => (x < a ? a : x > b ? b : x);
export const lerp = (a, b, k) => a + (b - a) * k;
export const invLerp = (a, b, x) => (b === a ? 0 : (x - a) / (b - a));
/** Progress of t through [a, b], clamped to 0..1. */
export const seg = (t, a, b) => clamp(invLerp(a, b, t));
export const smooth = (k) => { k = clamp(k); return k * k * (3 - 2 * k); };
/** 1 inside [a, b], with linear fades of fi / fo seconds at the edges. */
export const trap = (t, a, b, fi = 0.1, fo = 0.1) =>
  Math.min(fi > 0 ? clamp((t - a) / fi) : (t >= a ? 1 : 0), fo > 0 ? clamp((b - t) / fo) : (t < b ? 1 : 0));

export const ease = {
  linear: (k) => k,
  inQuad: (k) => k * k,
  outQuad: (k) => 1 - (1 - k) * (1 - k),
  inOutQuad: (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2),
  inCubic: (k) => k * k * k,
  outCubic: (k) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3),
  inOutCubic: (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 4 * k * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 3) / 2),
  outQuart: (k) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 4),
  inQuart: (k) => k * k * k * k,
  inExpo: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : Math.pow(2, 10 * k - 10)),
  outExpo: (k) => (k >= 1 ? 1 : 1 - Math.pow(2, -10 * k)),
  inOutExpo: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : k >= 1 ? 1 : k < 0.5 ? Math.pow(2, 20 * k - 10) / 2 : (2 - Math.pow(2, -20 * k + 10)) / 2),
  outBack: (k, s = 1.70158) => 1 + (s + 1) * Math.pow(k - 1, 3) + s * Math.pow(k - 1, 2),
  inBack: (k, s = 1.70158) => (s + 1) * k * k * k - s * k * k,
  outElastic: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : k >= 1 ? 1 : Math.pow(2, -10 * k) * Math.sin((k * 10 - 0.75) * (TAU / 3)) + 1),
  outBounce: (k) => {
    const n = 7.5625, d = 2.75;
    if (k < 1 / d) return n * k * k;
    if (k < 2 / d) return n * (k -= 1.5 / d) * k + 0.75;
    if (k < 2.5 / d) return n * (k -= 2.25 / d) * k + 0.9375;
    return n * (k -= 2.625 / d) * k + 0.984375;
  },
};
/** Eased progress of t through [a, b]. */
export const eseg = (t, a, b, fn = ease.outCubic) => fn(seg(t, a, b));

/**
 * Envelope that peaks (=1) at `at`: linear attack over `attack` seconds before it,
 * exponential decay with time constant `decay` after it. 0 far away.
 */
export function pulse(t, at, attack = 0.0, decay = 0.2) {
  const d = t - at;
  if (d < 0) return attack > 0 && d > -attack ? 1 + d / attack : 0;
  return Math.exp(-d / decay);
}

/** Integer hash -> [0, 1). Stable across runs and machines (Mulberry32 finalizer). */
export function hash(i, salt = 0) {
  let x = (Math.imul((i | 0) ^ 0x9e3779b9, 0x85ebca6b) ^ Math.imul(salt | 0, 0xc2b2ae35)) >>> 0;
  x = Math.imul(x ^ (x >>> 16), 0x7feb352d) >>> 0;
  x = Math.imul(x ^ (x >>> 15), 0x846ca68b) >>> 0;
  x = (x ^ (x >>> 16)) >>> 0;
  return x / 4294967296;
}
/** Signed hash in [-1, 1). */
