file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/feldman.js
// Oxytocin in new mothers and fathers (Gordon et al., 2010).
//
// One chart, "mothers" and "fathers", the two curves plotting left to right in step from
// "oxytocin's climbing" through the second line, fathers lower (what you would expect); on
// "identical in dads" the fathers' curve snaps exactly onto the mothers'. A cotton swab at the
// axis origin is drawn on "swabs" and drips on "spit"; the key reads "n = 80 couples" on
// "eighty". "n.s." is written between the two labels on "Fatherhood". The baby stands beside
// the chart watching the curve tips, wide-eyed on "dads", smiling on "biological".
//
// Every beat is keyed to a word and has a fallback time, so a take that rewords a line still
// plots the curves and snaps them (the snap falls back to the third line's start).

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, TAU } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, babyStandY, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const O = { x: 400, y: 712 };          // chart origin
const W = 900, H = 480;                // axis lengths
const X = (u) => O.x + u * W;
const Y = (v) => O.y - v * H;
// the curves in chart units (u: time 0..1, v: oxytocin 0..1); fathers start lower
const mothers = (u) => 0.12 + 0.7 * (1 - Math.exp(-2.7 * u)) + 0.022 * Math.sin(u * 8.5 + 0.4);
const fathersOff = (u) => 0.08 + 0.36 * (1 - Math.exp(-2.1 * u)) + 0.02 * Math.sin(u * 7.2 + 2.2);
const MARKS = [0.08, 0.36, 0.64, 0.92];
const ERR = [0.05, 0.06, 0.05, 0.07];
const BABY = { x: 1610, ground: 712, r: 64 };

/** First time that exists among the candidates (word lookups return Infinity when absent). */
const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();

    // ---- timing ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const tDay = first(C.wordTime('day'), C.start + 0.2);
    const tPlot0 = first(C.wordTime('oxytocins'), C.wordTime('oxytocin'), C.start + 1.6);
    const l3 = C.lineFor('identical') || C.lines[2] || null;
    const tSnap = first(C.wordTime('identical'), l3 ? l3.start : Infinity, C.end - 1.6);
    const tPlot1 = Math.min(first(C.wordTime('timings'), C.wordTime('timing'), tSnap - 0.4), tSnap - 0.2);
    const tSwab = first(C.wordTime('swabs'), C.wordTime('feldman'), tPlot0 + 0.8);
    const tSpit = first(C.wordTime('spit'), tSwab + 0.5);
    const tEighty = first(C.wordTime('eighty'), tSwab + 0.8);
    const tDads = first(C.wordTime('dads'), tSnap + 0.7);
    const tNS = first(C.wordTime('fatherhood'), tSnap + 1.0);
    const tBio = first(C.wordTime('biological'), tNS + 0.6);

    const pen = C.pen('chart', { width: 2.8 });
    const light = C.pen('light', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 });

    // ---- axes ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const pAx = C.inP;
    C.axes(pen, O.x, O.y, W + 60, H + 60, { p: pAx });
    text(ctx, 'time postpartum', O.x + W + 60, O.y + 48, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, C.start + 0.3, C.start + 0.9) });
    text(ctx, 'oxytocin', O.x - 18, O.y - H - 40, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, C.start + 0.35, C.start + 0.95) });
    // light grid lines inside the chart
    for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) light.line(O.x + 4, Y(i * 0.22), X(1.02), Y(i * 0.22), { p: seg(t, C.start + 0.1 + i * 0.06, C.start + 0.7 + i * 0.06), k: `gy${i}`, alpha: 0.55, width: 1.3 });
    // "day 1" at the origin, on "Day one"
    pen.line(X(0.0) + 1, O.y, X(0.0) + 1, O.y + 14, { p: seg(t, tDay, tDay + 0.15), k: 'tick-day', width: 2.2 });
    text(ctx, 'day 1', X(0.0) + 40, O.y + 46, { size: 28, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink, reveal: seg(t, tDay, tDay + 0.3) });

    // ---- the swab at the origin --------------------------------------------------------------
    drawSwab(ctx, C, seg(t, tSwab, tSwab + 0.45), seg(t, tSpit, tSpit + 0.5));

    // ---- the curves --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const plot = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, tPlot0, tPlot1));
    const snapK = t < tSnap ? 0 : Math.min(1.06, ease.outBack(seg(t, tSnap, tSnap + 0.24), 2.2));
    const fathers = (u) => lerp(fathersOff(u), mothers(u), snapK);
    const N = 64;
    const curve = (f) => Array.from({ length: N + 1 }, (_, i) => [X(i / N), Y(f(i / N))]);
    pen.poly(curve(mothers), { p: plot, k: 'mothers', width: 3.4 });
    pen.with({ width: 3.0 }).dashed(curve(fathers), { p: plot, k: 'fathers', dash: 16, gap: 9 });
    // markers and error bars, as the pen passes them
    MARKS.forEach((u, i) => {
      const mk = seg(plot, u - 0.015, u + 0.04);
      if (mk <= 0) return;
      for (const [f, kind] of [[mothers, 'm'], [fathers, 'f']]) {
        const x = X(u), y = Y(f(u)), e = ERR[i] * H * (kind === 'f' ? 0.9 : 1);
        light.line(x, y - e, x, y + e, { p: mk, k: `err${kind}${i}`, width: 1.6 });
        light.line(x - 8, y - e, x + 8, y - e, { p: mk, k: `errT${kind}${i}`, width: 1.6 });
        light.line(x - 8, y + e, x + 8, y + e, { p: mk, k: `errB${kind}${i}`, width: 1.6 });
        paperDisc(ctx, x, y, 11, mk);
        if (kind === 'm') pen.circle(x, y, 10, { p: mk, k: `mk${kind}${i}`, width: 2.6 });
        else pen.rect(x - 9, y - 9, 18, 18, { p: mk, k: `mk${kind}${i}`, width: 2.4, over: 1 });
      }
    });

    // ---- labels at the curve ends ---------------------------------------------------------------
    const endX = X(1) + 18;
    const mY = Y(mothers(1)), fY = Y(fathers(1));
    const lblP = seg(plot, 0.9, 1);
    text(ctx, 'mothers', endX, mY - 14, { size: 32, style: 'italic', reveal: lblP });
    // after the snap the fathers' label sits under the mothers'
    const fLblY = lerp(fY + 10, mY + 26, clamp(snapK));
    text(ctx, 'fathers', endX, fLblY, { size: 32, style: 'italic', reveal: lblP });
    // n.s.: a light bracket joining the two labels
    const nsP = seg(t, tNS, tNS + 0.4);
    if (nsP > 0) {
      const bx = endX + 128;
      light.poly([[bx, mY - 36], [bx + 10, mY - 36], [bx + 10, mY + 18], [bx, mY + 18]], { p: nsP, k: 'ns-br', width: 1.8 });
      text(ctx, 'n.s.', bx + 22, mY - 2, { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tNS + 0.15, tNS + 0.5) });
    }

    // ---- the key -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const kx = O.x + 48, ky = Y(1.0) + 10;
    const keyP = seg(t, tPlot0 - 0.1, tPlot0 + 0.5);
    pen.with({ width: 2.6 }).line(kx, ky, kx + 56, ky, { p: keyP, k: 'key-m' });
    pen.circle(kx + 28, ky, 9, { p: keyP, k: 'key-mc', width: 2.4 });
    text(ctx, 'mothers', kx + 72, ky + 10, { size: 28, color: P.ink, reveal: keyP });
    pen.with({ width: 2.4 }).dashed([[kx, ky + 44], [kx + 56, ky + 44]], { p: keyP, k: 'key-f', dash: 12, gap: 7 });
    pen.rect(kx + 20, ky + 36, 16, 16, { p: keyP, k: 'key-fs', width: 2.2, over: 1 });
    text(ctx, 'fathers', kx + 72, ky + 54, { size: 28, color: P.ink, reveal: keyP });
    text(ctx, 'n = 80 couples', kx, ky + 100, { size: 28, mono: true, color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tEighty, tEighty + 0.45) });

    // ---- the baby, watching the curve tips ------------------------------------------------------------
    const tipU = clamp(plot);
    const tip = [X(tipU), Y(lerp(mothers(tipU), fathers(tipU), 0.5))];
    const r = BABY.r, bx = BABY.x, by = babyStandY(BABY.ground, r);
    const dx = tip[0] - bx, dy = tip[1] - by, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const wow = clamp(seg(t, tDads - 0.05, tDads + 0.1)) * (1 - seg(t, tNS + 0.1, tNS + 0.5));
    const happy = seg(t, tBio, tBio + 0.25);
    const bob = Math.exp(-Math.max(0, t - tSnap) / 0.18) * (t >= tSnap ? 1 : 0);
    C.light = null;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= C.inP;
    // a short floor line under the baby
    light.line(bx - 110, BABY.ground + 2, bx + 110, BABY.ground + 2, { p: C.inP, k: 'floor', width: 1.6 });
    C.baby({
      x: bx, y: by - 10 * bob, r,
      eyes: wow > 0.5 ? 'wide' : 'open', open: blink(t, 11),
      look: [clamp(dx / d * 1.2, -1, 1), clamp(dy / d * 1.2, -1, 1)],
      mouth: happy > 0.5 ? 'smile' : 'o', mouthOpen: 0.25 + 0.55 * wow,
      brows: wow > 0.5 ? 'up' : null,
      tilt: -0.06 + 0.04 * Math.sin(t * 1.1),
      free: happy > 0 ? { mode: 'up', angle: t * 2.4 } : { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' },
      cap: { to: [-1.2, -3.2], sway: t * 0.8 },
    });
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

/** Paper under a marker (so the curve does not run through an open symbol). */
function paperDisc(ctx, x, y, r, a) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(a * 2);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

/** The cotton swab at the origin: a stick and a hatched bud; drip (0..1) lets a drop of spit fall. */
function drawSwab(ctx, C, p, drip) {
  if (p <= 0) return;
  const pen = C.pen('swab', { width: 2.6 });
  const bud = [O.x - 58, O.y + 46], end = [O.x - 170, O.y + 94];
  const ang = Math.atan2(bud[1] - end[1], bud[0] - end[0]);
  pen.with({ width: 3.2 }).line(end[0], end[1], bud[0] - Math.cos(ang) * 20, bud[1] - Math.sin(ang) * 20, { p: seg(p, 0, 0.6), k: 'stick' });
  const bp = seg(p, 0.45, 1);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= bp;
  ctx.translate(bud[0], bud[1]); ctx.rotate(ang);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 24, 13, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  const budPath = (c) => { c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(bud[0], bud[1], 24, 13, ang, 0, TAU); };
  pen.with({ width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch(budPath, { bbox: [bud[0] - 26, bud[1] - 26, 52, 52], gap: 6, angle: 0.9, p: bp, k: 'bud-h' });
  pen.ellipse(bud[0], bud[1], 24, 13, { p: bp, k: 'bud', rot: ang });
  text(ctx, 'swab', end[0] - 6, end[1] + 6, { size: 26, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, align: 'right', reveal: seg(p, 0.6, 1) });
  // a drop of spit falling from the bud
  if (drip > 0 && drip < 1) {
    const y = bud[1] + 16 + 30 * ease.inQuad(drip);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - seg(drip, 0.75, 1);
    pen.sub('drop').with({ width: 2 }).poly([[bud[0] + 6, y - 9], [bud[0] + 11, y], [bud[0] + 6, y + 5], [bud[0] + 1, y], [bud[0] + 6, y - 9]], { k: 'drop' });
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

#!/bin/zsh
# usage: shots.sh <name> <data-dir or -> t1,t2,... ; renders 540p stills and tiles them 2 wide into <name>.png

ins=""; for i in $(seq 0 $((n-1))); do ins="$ins[v$i]"; done

Writesongs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/abraham.js12:46 PM
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/abraham.js
// Amygdala response in primary-caregiving fathers (Abraham et al., 2014).
//
// One simple figure: a father's brain in side view (facing right, toward the screen), drawn in
// as the line starts, "n = 48 fathers" typed on "Forty-eight"; a screen on the right playing
// his own baby (the stimulus, the one coloured thing), a dotted arrow from it to the brain on
// "fathers"; on "amygdala" the almond outline is drawn and its hatching grows in as it is sung,
// cross-hatched with a few short rays on "lit"; "(mother-like)" joins the label on "data".

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, TAU } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const B = { x: 690, y: 470, s: 1.18 };   // the brain: centre and scale (facing right)
const at = (x, y) => [B.x + x * B.s, B.y + y * B.s];
// cerebrum outline (frontal pole on the right), cerebellum, brainstem, sulci; in brain units
const CEREBRUM = [[300, 20], [310, -60], [270, -150], [190, -215], [80, -245], [-40, -240], [-150, -215], [-240, -160], [-295, -80], [-305, 10], [-280, 70],
  [-225, 92], [-130, 104], [-40, 132], [60, 150], [140, 132], [178, 92], [170, 62], [210, 52], [262, 50], [300, 20]];
const CEREBELLUM = [[-140, 112], [-205, 104], [-268, 96], [-300, 130], [-272, 184], [-200, 202], [-148, 176], [-136, 140]];
const STEM = [[-40, 136], [-62, 262], [-108, 262], [-96, 132]];
const SULCI = [
  [[170, 62], [40, 2], [-80, -12], [-120, -30]],             // lateral fissure
  [[-20, -240], [0, -170], [-26, -100], [10, -18]],          // central sulcus
  [[120, -218], [134, -150], [176, -96]],
  [[-130, -196], [-110, -130], [-160, -70]],
  [[230, -40], [190, -40], [150, -70]],
  [[-230, -40], [-190, -10], [-224, 40]],
  [[40, 90], [-20, 70], [-90, 80]],
];
const AMY = { x: 120, y: 96, rx: 38, ry: 22, rot: 0.32 };
const SCREEN = { x: 1270, y: 262, w: 400, h: 290 };

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();

    const l0 = C.lines[0];
    const t48 = first(C.wordTime('forty eight'), C.wordTime('forty'), l0.start);
    const tFathers = first(C.wordTime('fathers'), t48 + 0.5);
    const tAmy = first(C.wordTime('amygdala'), tFathers + 0.35);
    const tLit = first(C.wordTime('lit'), tAmy + 0.45);
    const tData = first(C.wordTime('data'), tLit + 0.3);

    const pen = C.pen('brain', { width: 3.0, wave: 120 });
    const light = C.pen('sulci', { width: 2.0, color: P.ink2 });

    // ---- the brain --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const pBrain = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, C.start, Math.min(C.start + 0.9, tAmy - 0.1)));
    pen.spline(CEREBRUM.map(([x, y]) => at(x, y)), { p: pBrain, k: 'cerebrum', smooth: 10 });
    pen.spline(CEREBELLUM.map(([x, y]) => at(x, y)), { p: seg(pBrain, 0.3, 1), k: 'cerebellum' });
    pen.spline(STEM.map(([x, y]) => at(x, y)), { p: seg(pBrain, 0.45, 1), k: 'stem' });
    // the cerebellum's folia, light
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const y = 132 + i * 20;
      light.spline([at(-150, y), at(-210, y - 6), at(-276, y + 4 - i * 4)], { p: seg(pBrain, 0.55 + i * 0.1, 0.9 + i * 0.03), k: `fol${i}`, width: 1.6 });
    }
    SULCI.forEach((pts, i) => light.spline(pts.map(([x, y]) => at(x, y)), { p: seg(t, C.start + 0.25 + i * 0.07, C.start + 0.85 + i * 0.07), k: `sul${i}` }));
    text(ctx, 'n = 48 fathers', B.x - 300 * B.s, B.y + 290 * B.s, { size: 32, mono: true, reveal: seg(t, t48, t48 + 0.45) });
    text(ctx, 'primary caregivers', B.x - 300 * B.s, B.y + 290 * B.s + 40, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tFathers, tFathers + 0.5) });

    // ---- the screen with his baby -------------------------------------------------------------------
    const pScr = seg(t, C.start + 0.15, C.start + 0.75);
    const S = SCREEN;
    const sp = C.pen('screen', { width: 2.8 });
    sp.rect(S.x, S.y, S.w, S.h, { p: pScr, k: 'bezel' });
    C.pen('screen-in', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 }).rect(S.x + 16, S.y + 16, S.w - 32, S.h - 32, { p: pScr, k: 'glass' });
    sp.line(S.x + S.w / 2, S.y + S.h, S.x + S.w / 2, S.y + S.h + 54, { p: seg(pScr, 0.5, 1), k: 'neck' });
    sp.line(S.x + S.w / 2 - 70, S.y + S.h + 54, S.x + S.w / 2 + 70, S.y + S.h + 54, { p: seg(pScr, 0.6, 1), k: 'foot' });
    text(ctx, 'his own infant (video)', S.x + S.w / 2, S.y + S.h + 104, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, align: 'center', reveal: seg(pScr, 0.6, 1) });
    // the baby inside the glass
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(S.x + 17, S.y + 17, S.w - 34, S.h - 34); ctx.clip();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= seg(t, C.start + 0.35, C.start + 0.75);
    const r = 66, bx = S.x + S.w / 2, by = S.y + S.h / 2 + 4;
    const coo = Math.max(0, Math.sin((t - C.start) * 5.2));
    C.baby({ x: bx, y: by, r, eyes: 'open', open: blink(t, 23), look: [-0.7, 0.05], mouth: t >= tLit ? 'smile' : 'o', mouthOpen: 0.25 + 0.3 * coo,
      tilt: -0.05 + 0.04 * Math.sin(t * 2.1), free: { mode: 'wave', angle: t * 6 }, other: { mode: 'down' }, cap: { to: [2.4, -2.6], sway: t } });
    ctx.restore();
    // the dotted arrow from the screen to the brain
    const pArr = seg(t, tFathers - 0.05, tFathers + 0.35);
    if (pArr > 0) {
      const a0 = [S.x - 18, S.y + S.h * 0.55], a1 = at(318, 40);
      light.with({ color: P.ink }).dotted([a0, [lerp(a0[0], a1[0], 0.5), a0[1] + 6], a1], { p: pArr, k: 'arrow', gap: 13, r: 2.2 });
      if (pArr >= 1) light.with({ color: P.ink, width: 2.4 }).poly([[a1[0] + 18, a1[1] - 12], a1, [a1[0] + 20, a1[1] + 10]], { k: 'arrowhead' });
    }

    // ---- the amygdala ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const pAmy = seg(t, tAmy, tAmy + 0.3);
    if (pAmy > 0) {
      const [ax, ay] = at(AMY.x, AMY.y), rx = AMY.rx * B.s, ry = AMY.ry * B.s;
      const amyPath = (c) => { c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(ax, ay, rx, ry, AMY.rot, 0, TAU); };
      const box = [ax - rx - 4, ay - rx - 4, 2 * rx + 8, 2 * rx + 8];
      const hp = C.pen('amy-hatch', { width: 1.6 });
      hp.hatch(amyPath, { bbox: box, gap: 6, angle: -0.8, p: seg(t, tAmy + 0.05, tLit), k: 'h1' });
      hp.hatch(amyPath, { bbox: box, gap: 6, angle: 0.8, p: seg(t, tLit, tLit + 0.3), k: 'h2' });
      pen.ellipse(ax, ay, rx, ry, { p: pAmy, k: 'amy', rot: AMY.rot, width: 2.8 });
      // "lit": short rays around it
      const pRay = seg(t, tLit, tLit + 0.3);
      for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        const a = -Math.PI * 0.95 + (i / 6) * Math.PI * 0.9;
        const c = Math.cos(a), s = Math.sin(a);
        C.pen('rays').line(ax + c * (rx + 12), ay + s * (ry + 12), ax + c * (rx + 30), ay + s * (ry + 30), { p: seg(pRay, i * 0.08, 0.5 + i * 0.07), k: `ray${i}`, width: 2.2 });
      }
      // the leader and the label, below the brain's front
      const lead = [[ax + 10, ay + ry + 4], [ax + 70, ay + 150], [ax + 110, ay + 150]];
      light.poly(lead, { p: seg(t, tAmy + 0.1, tAmy + 0.4), k: 'leader', width: 1.6 });
      text(ctx, 'amygdala', ax + 120, ay + 160, { size: 34, style: 'italic', reveal: seg(t, tAmy + 0.15, tAmy + 0.5) });
      text(ctx, '(mother-like)', ax + 120, ay + 198, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tData, tData + 0.4) });
    }
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

Writesongs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/tronick.js
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/tronick.js
// We repair (Tronick et al., 1978).
//
// Three panels, a figure strip: a "play", b "still face", c "reunion". Each panel is adult
// Clawd (ink) standing face to face with the baby in an infant seat. Panel a draws in with the
// window: the adult leans in, talking, the baby smiles. Panel b draws in on "So he froze": on
// "froze" the adult's face goes flat (upright, mouth a line, arms down) and a stopwatch under
// the panel runs 0:00 to 2:00 by "Then she comes back" (song time is compressed: it reads 2:00
// when panel c starts). The baby watches, puzzled on "watched me", wobbles on "unglued", then
// cycles the bids as they are sung: smile, bid (a nub reaching for the adult), fuss, cry (pale
// blue tears), chew the hands. Panel c draws in on "Then": the adult leans back in and the
// baby settles, smiling by "repair"; on "repair" a light arrow runs under the strip from c
// back to a. Only the current panel's baby is in colour: the earlier panels' babies fade to
// ink copies, holding their last expression.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const PANELS = [{ x: 140, l: 'a', name: 'play' }, { x: 700, l: 'b', name: 'still face' }, { x: 1260, l: 'c', name: 'reunion' }];
const PW = 520, PY = 150, PH = 420, GROUND = 530;
const U = 27, BR = 60;
const ADX = 150, BBX = 404;                 // adult and baby x inside a panel
const EYE_Y = GROUND - 6.2 * U;              // adult eye height; the baby's face sits level with it

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };
const tearPhase = (t) => 0.001 + ((t * 0.9) % 1);

/** Everything time-like for the window, with fallbacks so every beat happens. */
function plan(C) {
  const L = C.lines;
  const l2 = C.lineFor('froze') || L[1] || null, l3 = C.lineFor('smile') || L[2] || null, l4 = C.lineFor('comes back') || L[3] || null;
  const tB = first(l2 ? l2.start : Infinity, C.start + (C.end - C.start) * 0.2);
  const tFroze = first(C.wordTime('froze'), tB + 1.2);
  const tThen = first(C.wordTime('then', { line: 'comes back' }), l4 ? l4.start : Infinity, C.end - 5);
  const tWatched = first(C.wordTime('watched'), lerp(tFroze, tThen, 0.25));
  const tUnglued = first(C.wordTime('unglued'), C.wordTime('come'), tWatched + 0.8);
  // the bids: on their words, else spread between "unglued" and "Then"
  const names = ['smile', 'bid', 'fuss', 'cry', 'chew'];
  const lo = l3 ? l3.start : tUnglued + 1, hi = tThen - 0.8;
  const bids = names.map((n, i) => first(C.wordTime(n, { line: 'smile' }), C.wordTime(n), lerp(lo, hi, i / names.length)));
  const tCope = first(C.wordTime('cope'), bids[4] + 1.2);
  const tBack = first(C.wordTime('back', { line: 'comes back' }), tThen + 0.9);
  const tRepair = first(C.wordTime('repair'), tBack + 0.6);
  const tHope = first(C.wordTime('hope'), tRepair + 2.5);
  return { tB, tFroze, tThen, tWatched, tUnglued, bids, tCope, tBack, tRepair, tHope };
}

/** The baby in panel b at time t (its expression cycles through the bids). */
function babyB(t, T) {
  const [tSmile, tBid, tFuss, tCry, tChew] = T.bids;
  const look = [-0.9, 0.05];
  if (t < T.tFroze) return { eyes: 'open', look, mouth: 'smile', free: { mode: 'wave', angle: t * 5 }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  if (t < T.tWatched) return { eyes: 'open', look, mouth: 'line', free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  if (t < T.tUnglued) return { eyes: 'open', look, mouth: 'line', brows: 'up', free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  if (t < tSmile) {
    const k = t - T.tUnglued;
    return { eyes: 'wide', look, mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.2, brows: 'up', tilt: 0.07 * Math.sin(k * 15) * Math.exp(-k / 0.7), free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  }
  if (t < tBid) return { eyes: 'open', look, mouth: 'grin', mouthOpen: 0.5, free: { mode: 'wave', angle: t * 7 }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  if (t < tFuss) {
    const k = ease.outBack(seg(t, tBid, tBid + 0.25));
    return { eyes: 'open', look: [-1, -0.1], mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.45, free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'hold', at: [lerp(-1.5, -2.25, k), lerp(0.8, -0.05, k)] }, tilt: -0.08 * k };
  }
  if (t < tCry) {
    const k = t - tFuss;
    return { eyes: 'open', look: [-0.6, 0.2], mouth: 'press', free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' }, tilt: 0.06 * Math.sin(k * 22) };
  }
  if (t < tChew) return { eyes: 'squeeze', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.95, tears: tearPhase(t), free: { mode: 'up', angle: t * 9 }, other: { mode: 'down' }, tilt: 0.04 * Math.sin((t - tCry) * 12) };
  // chew the hands to cope: both nubs at the mouth, eyes heavy, looking away by "cope"
  const away = seg(t, T.tCope - 0.2, T.tCope + 0.4);
  return { eyes: 'half', look: [lerp(0, 0.7, away), lerp(0.2, 0.6, away)], mouth: 'none', tears: tearPhase(t) * (1 - seg(t, T.tCope, T.tThen)) || 0,
    free: { mode: 'hold', at: [0.16, 0.56] }, other: { mode: 'hold', at: [-0.16, 0.56] }, tilt: 0.06 * away };
}

/** The baby in panel c: from the end of b to settled and smiling. */
function babyC(t, T) {
  const look = [-0.9, 0.05];
  if (t < T.tBack) return { eyes: 'half', look: [lerp(0.7, -0.6, seg(t, T.tThen + 0.3, T.tBack)), 0.3], mouth: 'none', tears: tearPhase(t) * 0.5,
    free: { mode: 'hold', at: [0.16, 0.56] }, other: { mode: 'hold', at: [-0.16, 0.56] } };
  if (t < T.tRepair) return { eyes: 'open', look, mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.2, brows: 'up', free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  const hope = seg(t, T.tHope, T.tHope + 0.3);
  return { eyes: 'open', look, mouth: hope > 0.5 ? 'grin' : 'smile', mouthOpen: 0.45, blush: 0.4 * hope,
    free: { mode: 'wave', angle: t * 5 }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();
    const T = plan(C);
    // when each panel draws in
    const pIn = [C.inP, ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tB, T.tB + 0.7)), ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tThen, T.tThen + 0.6))];
    const lights = C.pen('light', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 });

    PANELS.forEach((pn, i) => {
      const p = pIn[i];
      if (p <= 0) return;
      const pen = C.pen(`panel-${pn.l}`, { width: 2.6 });
      pen.rect(pn.x, PY, PW, PH, { p, k: 'frame' });
      text(ctx, pn.l, pn.x + 22, PY + 50, { size: 40, weight: 700, style: 'italic', alpha: clamp(p * 2) });
      text(ctx, pn.name, pn.x + 56, PY + 49, { size: 32, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 1) });
      lights.line(pn.x + 20, GROUND + 2, pn.x + PW - 20, GROUND + 2, { p, k: `floor${i}` });
      drawPanel(ctx, C, i, pn.x, p, T);
    });

    // ---- the stopwatch under panel b -----------------------------------------------------------
    const pT = seg(t, T.tFroze - 0.1, T.tFroze + 0.3);
    if (pT > 0) {
      const f = seg(t, T.tFroze, T.tThen);
      const secs = Math.floor(f * 120 + 1e-6);
      const cx = PANELS[1].x + PW / 2 - 60, cy = 650;
      const sw = C.pen('watch', { width: 2.4 });
      sw.circle(cx, cy, 24, { p: pT, k: 'dial' });
      sw.line(cx, cy - 26, cx, cy - 34, { p: pT, k: 'crown' });
      const a = -Math.PI / 2 + f * Math.PI * 4;  // the hand goes round twice
      sw.with({ width: 2 }).line(cx, cy, cx + Math.cos(a) * 18, cy + Math.sin(a) * 18, { p: pT, k: 'hand' });
      text(ctx, `${Math.floor(secs / 60)}:${String(secs % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`, cx + 40, cy + 15, { size: 44, mono: true, alpha: pT });
    }

    // ---- "repair": a light arrow from panel c back to panel a ------------------------------------
    const pR = seg(t, T.tRepair - 0.05, T.tRepair + 0.7);
    if (pR > 0) {
      const x0 = PANELS[2].x + PW / 2, x1 = PANELS[0].x + PW / 2, y0 = PY + PH + 14;
      const pts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= 40; i++) { const k = i / 40; pts.push([lerp(x0, x1, k), y0 + 26 + Math.sin(Math.PI * k) * 160]); }
      const ap = C.pen('repair', { width: 2.4 });
      ap.poly([[x0, y0], ...pts], { p: pR, k: 'arc' });
      if (pR >= 1) ap.poly([[x1 - 14, y0 + 48], [x1, y0 + 26], [x1 + 18, y0 + 44]], { k: 'head' });
      text(ctx, 'repair', (x0 + x1) / 2 + 300, y0 + 196, { size: 34, style: 'italic', reveal: seg(pR, 0.4, 1) });
    }
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

/** One panel: the adult and the baby in its seat, for panel i at time t. */
function drawPanel(ctx, C, i, px, p, T) {
  const t = C.t;
  // the adult: leaning in and talking (a, c), flat in b from "froze"
  let lean, mouth, arms, look;
  const talk = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 7.3 + i);
  if (i === 0) {
    const tt = Math.min(t, T.tB + 0.4);   // panel a holds still once panel b is drawn
    lean = 0.13 + 0.03 * Math.sin(tt * 2.2); mouth = 'o'; arms = [0.2, 0.75 + 0.15 * Math.sin(tt * 3.1)]; look = [0.8, 0.1];
  } else if (i === 1) {
    const flat = ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tFroze, T.tFroze + 0.18));
    const tt = Math.min(t, T.tFroze);
    lean = lerp(0.12 + 0.03 * Math.sin(tt * 2.2), 0, flat);
    mouth = flat > 0.5 ? 'line' : 'o';
    arms = [lerp(0.2, 0.08, flat), lerp(0.75, 0.08, flat)];
    look = [lerp(0.8, 0, flat), lerp(0.1, 0, flat)];
  } else {
    const back = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tThen + 0.4, T.tBack));
    lean = lerp(0, 0.14, back) + 0.025 * back * Math.sin(t * 2.2);
    mouth = back > 0.5 ? 'o' : 'line';
    arms = [lerp(0.08, 0.2, back), lerp(0.08, 0.8, back)];
    look = [lerp(0, 0.8, back), lerp(0, 0.1, back)];
  }
  C.adult({ x: px + ADX, y: GROUND, u: U, pose: 'stand', eyes: 'open', look, shine: P.paper, mouth, breath: talk, lean, arms, p, k: `p${i}` });

  // the infant seat on its stand (the back behind the baby)
  const bx = px + BBX, by = EYE_Y + 2;
  const sp = C.pen(`seat${i}`, { width: 2.4 });
  const footY = by + 1.9 * BR;
  sp.line(bx - 30, footY + 30, bx - 40, GROUND, { p, k: 'legL' });
  sp.line(bx + 30, footY + 30, bx + 40, GROUND, { p, k: 'legR' });
  sp.spline([[bx - 1.5 * BR, by + 0.5 * BR], [bx - 1.55 * BR, by + 1.5 * BR], [bx - 1.1 * BR, footY + 28], [bx + 1.1 * BR, footY + 28], [bx + 1.55 * BR, by + 1.5 * BR], [bx + 1.5 * BR, by + 0.5 * BR]], { p, k: 'shell' });

  // the baby: in colour while its panel is current, an ink copy once the next panel is drawn
  const toInk = i === 0 ? seg(t, T.tB, T.tB + 0.7) : i === 1 ? seg(t, T.tThen, T.tThen + 0.6) : 0;
  const tHold = i === 0 ? Math.min(t, T.tB) : i === 1 ? Math.min(t, T.tThen - 0.05) : t;
  let o;
  if (i === 0) o = { eyes: 'open', look: [-0.9, 0.05], mouth: 'grin', mouthOpen: 0.4 + 0.3 * Math.max(0, Math.sin(tHold * 4)), free: { mode: 'wave', angle: tHold * 5 }, other: { mode: 'down' } };
  else if (i === 1) o = babyB(tHold, T);
  else o = babyC(t, T);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 1.5);
  C.baby({ x: bx, y: by, r: BR, open: o.eyes === 'open' ? blink(tHold, 31 + i) : 1, ...o, tears: o.tears || 0, ink: toInk,
    cap: { to: [2.2, -2.2], sway: tHold, color: toInk > 0 ? undefined : P.cap } });
  ctx.restore();
  // the seat's front: paper with hatching over the legs (the baby sits in it)
  const fx = bx - 1.38 * BR, fy = by + 1.25 * BR, fw = 2.76 * BR, fh = footY + 28 - fy;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 1.5);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(fx, fy, fw, fh);
  ctx.restore();
  C.pen(`seat-h${i}`, { width: 1.3, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([fx, fy, fw, fh], { gap: 9, p, k: 'front-h' });
  sp.rect(fx, fy, fw, fh, { p, k: 'front' });
}

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/barburoth.js
// Air-stepping over optic flow, and the crawling skateboard (Barbu-Roth et al., 2009; Forma et al., 2019).
//
// Part one: the baby hangs in a harness (a beam, two straps, a hatched vest) over a floor in
// perspective, a light grid with a field of ink dots. A small atom is drawn on "nuclear
// physics"; on "hanging" the straps shorten and the baby lifts. On "Dots stream" the grid and
// the dots start to flow toward us (seeded dots, positions a pure function of the flow
// distance); on "I swim" the four legs start stepping in the air; on "astronaut" an inset
// with a tumbling ink astronaut is drawn top right; on "quadrupeds" a leader labels the legs.
// Part two, on "skateboard": the harness lifts out of frame, the floor fades, a mattress is
// drawn in plan view and the baby lies down along it, belly on a skateboard (head toward the
// far side), "age 2 d" on "two days old"; it pushes across with its legs from "two" to "rest",
// a dotted trail behind it; a tally of crossings gets a mark on "crossed", "Twice" and
// "Three"; on "rest" it stops and its eyes close.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, hash, TAU } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, blink, BABY_GEOM } from '../../kit.js';

// the floor in perspective: world X in [-1, 1], depth z in [Z0, Z1]
const FLOOR = { cx: 900, yH: 430, k: 370, kx: 620, Z0: 1, Z1: 5.5 };
const fx = (X, z) => FLOOR.cx + (X * FLOOR.kx) / z;
const fy = (z) => FLOOR.yH + FLOOR.k / z;
const HANG = { x: 900, y: 330, r: 70, lift: 42, beamY: 150 };
const MAT = { x: 430, y: 440, w: 1020, h: 340 };
const LIE = { y: 612, r: 62 };
const INSET = { x: 1390, y: 140, w: 330, h: 270 };
const NDOTS = 170;

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

function plan(C) {
  const L = C.lines;
  const tSk = first(C.wordTime('skateboard'), L[2] ? L[2].start + 1.6 : Infinity, C.end - 4);
  const l4 = C.lineFor('mattress') || L[3] || null;
  const tTwo = first(C.wordTime('two', { line: 'mattress' }), l4 ? l4.start : Infinity, tSk + 1);
  const tRest = first(C.wordTime('rest'), C.end - 0.5);
  const tCrossed = first(C.wordTime('crossed'), C.wordTime('mattress'), lerp(tTwo, tRest, 0.3));
  const tTwice = first(C.wordTime('twice'), tCrossed + 0.5);
  const tThree = first(C.wordTime('three', { line: 'mattress' }), tTwice + 0.3);
  return {
    tNuclear: first(C.wordTime('nuclear'), C.start + 0.8),
    tHang: first(C.wordTime('hanging'), C.start + 1.8),
    tDots: first(C.wordTime('dots'), L[1] ? L[1].start : Infinity, C.start + 3),
    tSwim: first(C.wordTime('swim'), C.wordTime('dots') + 0.8, C.start + 4),
    tAstro: first(C.wordTime('astronaut'), C.start + 4.7),
    tQuad: first(C.wordTime('quadrupeds'), L[2] ? L[2].start + 0.3 : Infinity, tSk - 1.5),
    tSk, tTwo, tCrossed, tTwice, tThree, tRest,
  };
}

/** Distance the floor has flowed toward us by t (speed ramps up over 0.5 s from tDots). */
function flowAt(t, t0, v = 1.25, ramp = 0.5) {
  const d = t - t0;
  if (d <= 0) return 0;
  return d < ramp ? (v * d * d) / (2 * ramp) : v * (d - ramp / 2);
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();
    const T = plan(C);
    const leave = ease.inCubic(seg(t, T.tSk, T.tSk + 0.6));   // the harness lifts away
    const partA = 1 - seg(t, T.tSk, T.tSk + 0.45);             // part one fades
    const flow = flowAt(t, T.tDots);

    // ---- part one: the floor, the atom, the astronaut inset -----------------------------------
    if (partA > 0) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha *= partA;
      drawFloor(ctx, C, flow, C.inP);
      drawAtom(ctx, C, seg(t, T.tNuclear, T.tNuclear + 0.5));
      drawInset(ctx, C, t, T.tAstro);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    // ---- part two: the mattress, the tally, the trail -----------------------------------------
    const pMat = ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tSk + 0.1, T.tSk + 0.9));
    const go = crossK(t, T);
    const x0 = MAT.x + 30 + 1.9 * LIE.r, x1 = MAT.x + MAT.w - 40 - 1.3 * LIE.r;
    const lieX = lerp(x0, x1, go);
    if (pMat > 0) {
      drawMattress(ctx, C, pMat);
      text(ctx, 'age 2 d', MAT.x + 26, MAT.y - 22, { size: 30, mono: true, reveal: seg(t, T.tTwo, T.tTwo + 0.35) });
      // the dotted trail behind the feet
      if (go > 0) C.pen('trail', { color: P.ink2 }).dotted([[x0 - 1.9 * LIE.r, LIE.y], [lieX - 1.9 * LIE.r, LIE.y]], { k: 'trail', gap: 14, r: 2.4 });
      drawTally(ctx, C, t, [T.tCrossed, T.tTwice, T.tThree], pMat);
    }

    // ---- the baby -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // part one: hanging (lifted on "hanging"); part two: carried to the mattress and laid down
    const lift = HANG.lift * ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tHang, T.tHang + 0.6));
    const down = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tSk + 0.1, T.tSk + 0.85));
    const r = lerp(HANG.r, LIE.r, down);
    const bx = lerp(HANG.x, lieX, down), by = lerp(HANG.y - lift, LIE.y, down);
    const tilt = lerp(0, Math.PI / 2, down);
    // legs: dangling, stepping from "I swim", pushing on the board while it crosses
    const swim = seg(t, T.tSwim, T.tSwim + 0.3) * (1 - down);
    const push = down * (go > 0 && t < T.tRest ? 1 : 0);
    const legs = (i) => {
      const ph = t * TAU * 1.7 + (i % 2 ? Math.PI : 0) + (i > 1 ? 0.5 : 0);
      const dangle = 0.06 * Math.sin(t * 2.1 + i);
      const a = dangle * (1 - swim) + swim * 0.28 * Math.sin(ph) * (i < 2 ? 1 : -1) + push * 0.35 * Math.sin(t * TAU * 2.2 + (i % 2 ? Math.PI : 0));
      const len = 1 - swim * 0.32 * Math.max(0, Math.sin(ph + 1.2)) - push * 0.2 * Math.max(0, Math.sin(t * TAU * 2.2 + (i % 2 ? 0 : Math.PI)));
      return { a, len };
    };
    const rest = t >= T.tRest;
    const lookA = t >= T.tAstro && t < T.tQuad ? [0.9, -0.7] : t >= T.tDots ? [0.1, 0.7] : [0, 0.3];
    const face = rest ? { eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'line' }
      : down > 0.5 ? { eyes: 'focus', mouth: 'press', look: [0, 0] }
      : { eyes: swim > 0.5 ? 'wide' : 'open', open: blink(t, 41), mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.3 + 0.3 * swim, look: lookA };

    // the skateboard under the lying baby (in its frame)
    if (down > 0) drawBoard(ctx, C, bx, by, r, tilt, seg(t, T.tSk + 0.4, T.tSk + 0.9));
    // the harness straps behind the vest, lifting away on "skateboard"
    const upY = -560 * leave;
    if (leave < 1) drawStraps(ctx, C, bx, by, r, upY, C.inP);
    babyWithLegs(ctx, C, {
      x: bx, y: by, r, tilt, ...face,
      free: down > 0.5 ? { mode: 'hold', at: [1.55, -0.2] } : { mode: swim > 0.5 ? 'up' : 'down', angle: t * 4 },
      other: down > 0.5 ? { mode: 'hold', at: [-1.55, -0.2] } : { mode: 'down' },
      cap: { to: down > 0.5 ? [3.2, -1.2] : [-2.2, -3.0], sway: t },
    }, legs);
    if (leave < 1) drawVest(ctx, C, bx, by, r, upY, C.inP);
    if (down > 0) drawBoardStrap(ctx, C, bx, by, r, tilt, seg(t, T.tSk + 0.7, T.tSk + 1.0));

    // "quadrupeds": a leader to the four legs
    const pQ = seg(t, T.tQuad, T.tQuad + 0.4) * partA;
    if (pQ > 0) {
      const footY = HANG.y - HANG.lift + 1.9 * HANG.r;
      const lp = C.pen('quad', { width: 1.8, color: P.ink2 });
      lp.poly([[HANG.x - 1.05 * HANG.r, footY + 10], [HANG.x - 1.05 * HANG.r, footY + 22], [HANG.x + 1.05 * HANG.r, footY + 22], [HANG.x + 1.05 * HANG.r, footY + 10]], { p: pQ, k: 'brace' });
      lp.line(HANG.x + 1.05 * HANG.r, footY + 22, HANG.x + 1.05 * HANG.r + 90, footY + 40, { p: seg(pQ, 0.4, 1), k: 'lead' });
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= partA;
      text(ctx, 'quadruped', HANG.x + 1.05 * HANG.r + 100, footY + 50, { size: 34, style: 'italic', reveal: seg(t, T.tQuad + 0.15, T.tQuad + 0.55) });
      text(ctx, '4 limbs stepping', HANG.x + 1.05 * HANG.r + 100, footY + 88, { size: 28, mono: true, color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, T.tQuad + 0.3, T.tQuad + 0.7) });
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

/** 0..1 across the mattress, in seven leg pushes from "two" to "rest". */
function crossK(t, T) {
  const a = Math.max(T.tTwo, T.tSk + 0.85), b = Math.max(T.tRest, a + 0.5);
  const k = seg(t, a, b), n = 7;
  const i = Math.min(n - 1, Math.floor(k * n)), f = k * n - i;
  return (i + ease.outQuad(f)) / n;
}

// ---- part one -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function drawFloor(ctx, C, flow, p) {
  const { Z0, Z1 } = FLOOR;
  const edge = C.pen('floor', { width: 2.4 });
  const grid = C.pen('grid', { width: 1.4, color: P.ink2 });
  // the outline of the floor plane
  edge.poly([[fx(-1, Z1), fy(Z1)], [fx(1, Z1), fy(Z1)]], { p, k: 'far' });
  edge.poly([[fx(-1, Z0), fy(Z0)], [fx(-1, Z1), fy(Z1)]], { p, k: 'left' });
  edge.poly([[fx(1, Z0), fy(Z0)], [fx(1, Z1), fy(Z1)]], { p, k: 'right' });
  for (let i = 1; i < 8; i++) {
    const X = -1 + i / 4;
    grid.line(fx(X, Z0), fy(Z0), fx(X, Z1), fy(Z1), { p, k: `gx${i}`, alpha: 0.8 });
  }
  // cross lines at a 0.5 spacing, flowing toward us
  const sp = 0.5, off = flow % sp;
  ctx.save();
  for (let j = 0; j < 10; j++) {
    const z = Z1 - ((j * sp + off) % (Z1 - Z0));
    if (z <= Z0 + 0.02) continue;
    const a = clamp((Z1 - z) / 0.4) * clamp((z - Z0) / 0.15);
    ctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.8 * p;
    grid.line(fx(-1, z), fy(z), fx(1, z), fy(z), { k: `gz${j}` });
  }
  ctx.restore();
  // the dots: seeded positions on the floor, carried toward us by the flow
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  for (let i = 0; i < NDOTS; i++) {
    const X = (hash(i, 801) * 2 - 1) * 0.96;
    const z = Z1 - ((hash(i, 802) * (Z1 - Z0) + flow) % (Z1 - Z0));
    const a = clamp((Z1 - z) / 0.5) * clamp((z - Z0) / 0.2);
    if (a <= 0) continue;
    ctx.globalAlpha = a * p * (0.7 + 0.3 * hash(i, 803));
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(fx(X, z), fy(z), (8.5 / z) * (0.8 + 0.4 * hash(i, 804)), (5 / z) * (0.8 + 0.4 * hash(i, 804)), 0, 0, TAU);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  // a hatched shadow under the hanging baby
  const sz = 2.3;
  const shadow = (c) => { c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(HANG.x, fy(sz), 120, 20, 0, 0, TAU); };
  C.pen('shadow', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch(shadow, { bbox: [HANG.x - 124, fy(sz) - 24, 248, 48], gap: 7, angle: -0.6, p, k: 'shadow' });
}

function drawAtom(ctx, C, p) {
  if (p <= 0) return;
  const pen = C.pen('atom', { width: 2.2 });
  const cx = 290, cy = 220;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) pen.ellipse(cx, cy, 54, 18, { p: seg(p, i * 0.15, 0.55 + i * 0.15), rot: (i * Math.PI) / 3, k: `orbit${i}` });
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= p; ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, 7, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  text(ctx, 'nuclear physics', cx, cy + 66, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, align: 'center', reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 1) });
}

/** The inset: a tumbling astronaut in ink, drawn on "astronaut". */
function drawInset(ctx, C, t, t0) {
  const p = seg(t, t0 - 0.1, t0 + 0.35);
  if (p <= 0) return;
  const I = INSET;
  const pen = C.pen('inset', { width: 2.4 });
  pen.rect(I.x, I.y, I.w, I.h, { p, k: 'box' });
  text(ctx, 'astronaut', I.x + 14, I.y + I.h - 16, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(I.x + 2, I.y + 2, I.w - 4, I.h - 4); ctx.clip();
  // stars
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink2;
  for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = p * (0.5 + 0.5 * hash(i, 901));
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(I.x + 14 + hash(i, 902) * (I.w - 28), I.y + 14 + hash(i, 903) * (I.h - 60), 1.6 + 1.6 * hash(i, 904), 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  const dt = Math.max(0, t - t0);
  ctx.translate(I.x + I.w / 2 + 10 * Math.sin(dt * 0.9), I.y + I.h / 2 - 8 + 6 * Math.sin(dt * 1.3));
  ctx.rotate(0.5 + dt * 1.1);
  ctx.scale(1.15, 1.15);
  const ap = seg(p, 0.3, 1);
  const a = C.pen('astronaut', { width: 2.2 });
  // backpack, body, helmet with a hatched visor, arms, legs
  a.rrect(-34, -18, 14, 50, 4, { p: ap, k: 'pack' });
  paperRR(ctx, -24, -20, 48, 58, 12, ap);
  a.rrect(-24, -20, 48, 58, 12, { p: ap, k: 'body' });
  paperCircle(ctx, 0, -46, 27, ap);
  a.circle(0, -46, 27, { p: ap, k: 'helmet' });
  const visor = (c) => { c.beginPath(); c.ellipse(5, -46, 17, 12, 0, 0, TAU); };
  C.pen('visor', { width: 1.2 }).hatch(visor, { bbox: [-14, -60, 38, 28], gap: 5, angle: -0.7, p: ap, k: 'vh' });
  a.ellipse(5, -46, 17, 12, { p: ap, k: 'visor' });
  a.line(22, -8, 48, -30, { p: ap, k: 'armR' });
  a.line(-22, -6, -50, 14, { p: ap, k: 'armL' });
  a.circle(51, -33, 6, { p: ap, k: 'mitR' });
  a.circle(-53, 17, 6, { p: ap, k: 'mitL' });
  a.line(-10, 38, -20, 76, { p: ap, k: 'legL' });
  a.line(10, 38, 26, 72, { p: ap, k: 'legR' });
  a.rect(-30, 74, 16, 9, { p: ap, k: 'bootL', over: 1 });
  a.rect(22, 70, 16, 9, { p: ap, k: 'bootR', over: 1 });
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawStraps(ctx, C, bx, by, r, upY, p) {
  const pen = C.pen('harness', { width: 2.6 });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(0, upY);
  const bw = 1.55 * r;
  pen.line(HANG.x - bw - 40, HANG.beamY, HANG.x + bw + 40, HANG.beamY, { p, k: 'beam' });
  pen.line(HANG.x, HANG.beamY, HANG.x, HANG.beamY - 60, { p, k: 'rod' });
  ctx.restore();
  // the straps run from the beam down to the vest (which rides on the baby until it lifts)
  const vy = by + 0.72 * r + upY;
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) pen.line(HANG.x + k * (bw + 10), HANG.beamY + upY, bx + k * 1.42 * r, vy, { p, k: `strap${k}`, width: 2.2 });
}

function drawVest(ctx, C, bx, by, r, upY, p) {
  const y = by + 0.62 * r + upY, h = 0.34 * r, x = bx - 1.36 * r, w = 2.72 * r;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 1.5);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
  ctx.restore();
  C.pen('vest-h', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([x, y, w, h], { gap: 7, angle: 0.8, p, k: 'vh' });
  C.pen('vest', { width: 2.4 }).rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'vest' });
}

// ---- part two ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function drawMattress(ctx, C, p) {
  const M = MAT;
  const pen = C.pen('mattress', { width: 2.8 });
  const light = C.pen('tufts', { width: 1.5, color: P.ink2 });
  pen.rrect(M.x, M.y, M.w, M.h, 34, { p, k: 'edge' });
  light.rrect(M.x + 18, M.y + 18, M.w - 36, M.h - 36, 22, { p, k: 'piping' });
  // tufting buttons: little crosses on a grid
  let n = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    const x = M.x + 80 + i * ((M.w - 160) / 7), y = M.y + 70 + j * ((M.h - 140) / 2);
    const q = seg(p, 0.3 + (n++ / 24) * 0.6, 0.4 + (n / 24) * 0.6);
    light.line(x - 7, y - 7, x + 7, y + 7, { p: q, k: `tA${i},${j}` });
    light.line(x - 7, y + 7, x + 7, y - 7, { p: q, k: `tB${i},${j}` });
  }
  text(ctx, 'mattress, plan view', M.x + M.w - 24, M.y + M.h + 44, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, align: 'right', reveal: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
}

function drawTally(ctx, C, t, times, p) {
  const x = MAT.x + MAT.w - 300, y = MAT.y - 104;
  text(ctx, 'crossings', x, y + 36, { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: p });
  const pen = C.pen('tally', { width: 3.2 });
  times.forEach((t0, i) => pen.line(x + 160 + i * 24, y - 4, x + 156 + i * 24, y + 46, { p: seg(t, t0, t0 + 0.12), k: `mark${i}` }));
}

/** The skateboard deck and wheels under the lying baby (drawn in the baby's frame). */
function drawBoard(ctx, C, bx, by, r, tilt, p) {
  if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(bx, by); ctx.rotate(tilt);
  const pen = C.pen('board', { width: 2.6 });
  for (const [wx, wy] of [[-1.2, -0.2], [1.04, -0.2], [-1.2, 1.25], [1.04, 1.25]]) pen.rect(wx * r, wy * r, 0.16 * r, 0.32 * r, { p, k: `wheel${wx},${wy}`, over: 1 });
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-1.08 * r, -0.55 * r, 2.16 * r, 2.25 * r, 0.5 * r); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  C.pen('board-h', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch((c) => { c.beginPath(); c.roundRect(-1.08 * r, -0.55 * r, 2.16 * r, 2.25 * r, 0.5 * r); }, { bbox: [-1.1 * r, -0.6 * r, 2.2 * r, 2.35 * r], gap: 9, angle: 0.3, p, k: 'deck-h' });
  pen.rrect(-1.08 * r, -0.55 * r, 2.16 * r, 2.25 * r, 0.5 * r, { p, k: 'deck' });
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawBoardStrap(ctx, C, bx, by, r, tilt, p) {
  if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(bx, by); ctx.rotate(tilt);
  const pen = C.pen('boardstrap', { width: 2.4 });
  pen.line(-1.42 * r, 0.98 * r, 1.42 * r, 0.98 * r, { p, k: 's1' });
  pen.line(-1.42 * r, 1.18 * r, 1.42 * r, 1.18 * r, { p, k: 's2' });
  ctx.restore();
}

// ---- the baby with posable legs ------------------------------------------------------------

/** The body outline of baby Clawd (as shared/clawd/baby.js draws it), for clipping off its legs. */
function bodyPath(ctx, r) {
  const { l, r: rt, t, b, rad, bulge } = BABY_GEOM.body;
  const L = l * r, R = rt * r, T = t * r, B = b * r, k = rad * r, q = bulge * r;
  ctx.moveTo(L + k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, T - q, R - k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, T, R, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R + q, (T + B) / 2, R, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, B, R - k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, B + q, L + k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, B, L, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L - q, (T + B) / 2, L, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, T, L + k, T);
  ctx.closePath();
}

/**
 * The clay baby with its four legs posed by legs(i) -> { a (radians about the hip), len (x the
 * resting length) }: our legs are drawn first, then the baby with its own legs clipped away
 * (the clip is everything above its hips plus its body outline, in its tilted frame).
 */
function babyWithLegs(ctx, C, o, legs) {
  const r = o.r, G = BABY_GEOM;
  const lw = r * 0.034 * 0.9;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(o.x, o.y); ctx.rotate(o.tilt || 0);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.skinShade; ctx.strokeStyle = P.ink; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const { a, len } = legs(i);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(G.legs[i] * r, (G.body.b - 0.14) * r);
    ctx.rotate(a);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-(G.legW / 2) * r, 0, G.legW * r, (0.14 + G.legH + 0.02) * r * len + 0.06 * r, 0.08 * r);
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.rect(-8 * r, -10 * r, 16 * r, 11.15 * r);
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.rotate(-(o.tilt || 0)); ctx.translate(-o.x, -o.y);
  ctx.clip();
  C.baby({ ...o });
  ctx.restore();
}

function paperRR(ctx, x, y, w, h, r, a) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(a * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(x, y, w, h, r); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
function paperCircle(ctx, x, y, r, a) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(a * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/lampl.js
// Growth saltation in one infant (Lampl, 1992).
//
// Length (cm) against age (days). The classic smooth growth curve in light ink, drawn with
// the axes; the real data as an ink staircase plotting left to right, measurement points on
// the flats. The first four points type the notebook row 59.8 / 59.8 / 59.8 / 60.8 (top left,
// a light leader to the first step); the staircase steps to 62.7 on "sixty-two" and to 64.5
// on "sixty-four" (the labels roll); on "two and a half centimeters" a 2.5 cm step rises with
// a dimension arrow, "overnight" beside it; "sixty-three days" draws the long flat run with a
// bracket under it. The baby stands on the current step, walking behind the pen and hopping
// up each step, a few pixels taller after every one.
//
// Optional beats, each only when its line is in the take: "I'm terrible at this" circles the
// notebook row with a "?", struck through on "Nope" with a tick; "Ninety-five percent" hatches
// the growth-free days along the axis; "Front page" stamps a newspaper in on the beat and a
// big name's speech bubble ("measurement error!") is struck through on "mocked her". The core
// (staircase, steps, flat run, baby) only needs the first and third lines.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, babyStandY, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const O = { x: 330, y: 760 };
const DAYS = 150, CM0 = 59, CM1 = 68;
const X = (d) => O.x + d * (1300 / DAYS);
const Y = (cm) => O.y - (cm - CM0) * (560 / (CM1 - CM0));
// the staircase: vertices (day, cm)
const V = [[0, 59.8], [24, 59.8], [24, 60.8], [40, 60.8], [40, 62.7], [58, 62.7], [58, 64.5], [78, 64.5], [78, 67.0], [141, 67.0]];
const RISERS = [2, 4, 6, 8];               // vertex index at the top of each step
// measurement points: the notebook's four, then every 8 days along each flat
const PTS = (() => {
  const out = [[0, 59.8], [8, 59.8], [16, 59.8], [24, 60.8], [32, 60.8]];
  for (const [a, b, cm] of [[40, 58, 62.7], [58, 78, 64.5], [78, 141, 67.0]]) for (let d = a; d < b; d += 8) out.push([d, cm]);
  return out;
})();
const smooth = (d) => 59.4 + 8.4 * (1 - Math.exp(-d / 90)) / (1 - Math.exp(-DAYS / 90));
const ROW = { x: 360, y: 182, size: 32 };
const PAPER = { x: 372, y: 228, w: 340, h: 212 };

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

function plan(C) {
  const L = C.lines;
  const l3 = C.lineFor('centimeters') || L.find((l) => /overnight|sixty three days/.test(l.n)) || L[1] || null;
  const t0 = first(C.wordTime('lampls'), C.wordTime('lampl'), L[0] ? L[0].start : Infinity, C.start + 0.2);
  const t62 = first(C.wordTime('sixty two'), t0 + 0.9);
  const t627 = first(C.wordTime('seven', { line: 'sixty two' }), t62 + 0.6);
  const t64 = first(C.wordTime('sixty four'), t62 + 1.3);
  const t645 = first(C.wordTime('five', { line: 'sixty four' }), t64 + 0.6);
  const tStep = first(C.wordTime('two', { line: 'centimeters' }), l3 ? l3.start + 0.25 : Infinity, t64 + 3);
  const tCm = first(C.wordTime('centimeters'), tStep + 0.45);
  const tOver = first(C.wordTime('overnight'), tCm + 0.5);
  const t63 = first(C.wordTime('sixty three'), tOver + 0.5);
  const tDays = first(C.wordTime('days', { line: 'sixty three' }), t63 + 0.5);
  const tFlat = first(C.wordTime('flat'), tDays + 0.4);
  // keyframes: (time, position along the staircase in vertex units)
  const K = [[t0 + 0.05, 0], [t62 - 0.32, 1], [t62 - 0.24, 2], [t62 - 0.06, 3], [t62 + 0.1, 4], [t64 - 0.06, 5], [t64 + 0.1, 6],
    [Math.min(t64 + 0.8, tStep - 0.1), 7], [tStep, 7], [tCm + 0.1, 8], [t63, 8], [tFlat + 0.3, 9]];
  for (let i = 1; i < K.length; i++) K[i][0] = Math.max(K[i][0], K[i - 1][0] + 0.01);
  return {
    t0, t62, t627, t64, t645, tStep, tCm, tOver, t63, tDays, tFlat, K,
    tTerrible: C.wordTime('terrible'), tThis: C.wordTime('this', { line: 'terrible' }), tNope: C.wordTime('nope'), tArrive: C.wordTime('arrive'),
    t95: C.wordTime('ninety five'), tFree: C.wordTime('growth free'),
    tFront: C.onBeat(C.wordTime('front')), tBig: C.wordTime('big'), tName: C.wordTime('name', { line: 'front page' }),
    tMocked: C.wordTime('mocked'), tHer: C.wordTime('her', { line: 'mocked' }),
  };
}

/** Position along the staircase (vertex units) at t. */
function posAt(t, K) {
  if (t <= K[0][0]) return K[0][1];
  for (let i = 1; i < K.length; i++) if (t < K[i][0]) return lerp(K[i - 1][1], K[i][1], seg(t, K[i - 1][0], K[i][0]));
  return K.at(-1)[1];
}
const vAt = (pos) => {
  const i = Math.min(V.length - 2, Math.floor(pos)), f = pos - i;
  return [lerp(V[i][0], V[i + 1][0], f), lerp(V[i][1], V[i + 1][1], f)];
};
/** The time the staircase reaches day d on its flats (for typing the notebook, the points). */
function timeAtDay(d, cm, K) {
  // find the flat segment holding (d, cm) and invert the keyframes there
  for (let i = 0; i < V.length - 1; i++) {
    const [d0, c0] = V[i], [d1, c1] = V[i + 1];
    if (c0 === c1 && c0 === cm && d >= d0 && d <= d1) {
      const pos = i + (d1 > d0 ? (d - d0) / (d1 - d0) : 0);
      for (let k = 1; k < K.length; k++) if (pos <= K[k][1] && K[k][1] > K[k - 1][1]) return lerp(K[k - 1][0], K[k][0], (pos - K[k - 1][1]) / (K[k][1] - K[k - 1][1]));
    }
  }
  return Infinity;
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();
    const T = plan(C);
    const pen = C.pen('chart', { width: 2.8 });
    const light = C.pen('light', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 });

    // ---- axes, ticks and the smooth curve ------------------------------------------------------
    const pAx = C.inP;
    C.axes(pen, O.x, O.y, 1330, 600, { p: pAx });
    text(ctx, 'age (days)', O.x + 1330, O.y + 50, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, C.start + 0.3, C.start + 0.9) });
    text(ctx, 'length (cm)', O.x - 16, Y(CM1) - 36, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, C.start + 0.3, C.start + 0.9) });
    for (const cm of [60, 62, 64, 66, 68]) {
      light.line(O.x - 12, Y(cm), O.x, Y(cm), { p: pAx, k: `ty${cm}`, width: 2 });
      text(ctx, String(cm), O.x - 20, Y(cm) + 9, { size: 26, mono: true, align: 'right', color: P.ink2, alpha: pAx });
    }
    for (const d of [30, 60, 90, 120, 150]) {
      light.line(X(d), O.y, X(d), O.y + 12, { p: pAx, k: `tx${d}`, width: 2 });
      text(ctx, String(d), X(d), O.y + 40, { size: 26, mono: true, align: 'center', color: P.ink2, alpha: pAx });
    }
    const curve = Array.from({ length: 61 }, (_, i) => [X((i / 60) * DAYS), Y(smooth((i / 60) * DAYS))]);
    light.poly(curve, { p: ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, C.start + 0.15, T.t0 + 0.8)), k: 'smooth', width: 2.2 });
    text(ctx, 'expected', X(118), Y(smooth(118)) + 42, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, T.t0 + 0.6, T.t0 + 1.1) });

    // ---- the growth-free days (optional: "Ninety-five percent") ----------------------------------
    if (isFinite(T.t95)) {
      const pf = seg(t, T.t95, first(T.tFree, T.t95 + 1) + 0.2);
      const hp = C.pen('free', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 });
      const gaps = [24, 40, 58, 78];
      let d0 = 0;
      [...gaps, 141].forEach((d1, i) => {
        const a = X(d0) + (i ? 5 : 0), b = X(d1) - 5;
        const q = seg(pf, (d0 / 141), (d1 / 141));
        if (q > 0) hp.hatch([a, O.y - 26, (b - a) * q, 18], { gap: 6, angle: -0.9, k: `free${i}` });
        d0 = d1;
      });
      text(ctx, 'no growth on 95 % of days', X(141), O.y - 40, { size: 28, mono: true, align: 'right', reveal: seg(t, first(T.tFree, T.t95 + 1), first(T.tFree, T.t95 + 1) + 0.4) });
    }

    // ---- the staircase and its points ------------------------------------------------------------
    const pos = posAt(t, T.K);
    const S = V.map(([d, cm]) => [X(d), Y(cm)]);
    let total = 0; const acc = [0];
    for (let i = 1; i < S.length; i++) { total += Math.hypot(S[i][0] - S[i - 1][0], S[i][1] - S[i - 1][1]); acc.push(total); }
    const iP = Math.min(S.length - 2, Math.floor(pos));
    const sNow = acc[iP] + (acc[iP + 1] - acc[iP]) * (pos - iP);
    pen.poly(S, { p: sNow / total, k: 'stairs', width: 3.6 });
    PTS.forEach(([d, cm], i) => {
      const tp = timeAtDay(d, cm, T.K);
      const k = seg(t, tp - 0.02, tp + 0.08);
      if (k <= 0) return;
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= k; ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(X(d), Y(cm), 7, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
      pen.circle(X(d), Y(cm), 6.5, { p: k, k: `pt${i}`, width: 2.4 });
    });

    // ---- the notebook row (typed as its four points are plotted) -------------------------------------
    const entries = ['59.8', '59.8', '59.8', '60.8'];
    let x = ROW.x;
    text(ctx, 'notebook', ROW.x, ROW.y - 40, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, T.t0, T.t0 + 0.3) });
    entries.forEach((s, i) => {
      const tp = timeAtDay(PTS[i][0], PTS[i][1], T.K);
      const rv = seg(t, tp, tp + 0.12);
      x += text(ctx, s, x, ROW.y, { size: ROW.size, mono: true, reveal: rv });
      if (i < 3) x += text(ctx, ' / ', x, ROW.y, { size: ROW.size, mono: true, color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tp + 0.1, tp + 0.16) });
    });
    const rowEnd = x;
    // a light dotted leader from the row's last entry to its point
    const tp3 = timeAtDay(24, 60.8, T.K);
    light.dotted([[rowEnd - 40, ROW.y + 12], [X(24) + 6, Y(60.8) - 16]], { p: seg(t, tp3 + 0.1, tp3 + 0.5), k: 'rowlead', gap: 12, r: 1.8 });

    // optional: "I'm terrible at this": the row circled, a "?"; "Nope": struck, a tick
    if (isFinite(T.tTerrible)) {
      const np = C.pen('nope', { width: 2.4 });
      np.ellipse((ROW.x + rowEnd) / 2 - 6, ROW.y - 11, (rowEnd - ROW.x) / 2 + 28, 34, { p: seg(t, T.tTerrible, T.tTerrible + 0.4), k: 'circle', rot: -0.02 });
      const qx = rowEnd + 46;
      text(ctx, '?', qx, ROW.y + 4, { size: 54, style: 'italic', weight: 700, reveal: seg(t, first(T.tThis, T.tTerrible + 0.5), first(T.tThis, T.tTerrible + 0.5) + 0.2) });
      if (isFinite(T.tNope)) {
        np.line(qx - 12, ROW.y + 10, qx + 34, ROW.y - 40, { p: seg(t, T.tNope, T.tNope + 0.15), k: 'strike', width: 3 });
        np.poly([[qx + 56, ROW.y - 18], [qx + 70, ROW.y], [qx + 100, ROW.y - 44]], { p: seg(t, T.tNope + 0.15, T.tNope + 0.4), k: 'tick', width: 3.2 });
      }
    }

    // ---- step labels ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const roll = (a, b, k) => (lerp(a, b, ease.outCubic(k))).toFixed(1);
    const k62 = seg(t, T.t62, T.t627 + 0.1), k64 = seg(t, T.t64, T.t645 + 0.1);
    if (t >= T.t62) text(ctx, roll(60.8, 62.7, k62), X(40) - 14, Y(62.7) - 16, { size: 32, mono: true, align: 'right' });
    if (t >= T.t64) text(ctx, roll(62.7, 64.5, k64), X(58) - 14, Y(64.5) - 16, { size: 32, mono: true, align: 'right' });
    // the 2.5 cm step: a dimension arrow and "overnight"
    const pDim = seg(t, T.tCm, T.tCm + 0.35);
    if (pDim > 0) {
      const dx = X(78) + 34, ya = Y(64.5), yb = Y(67.0);
      const dp = C.pen('dim', { width: 2 });
      dp.line(dx, ya, dx, yb, { p: pDim, k: 'dim' });
      for (const [yy, s] of [[ya, 1], [yb, -1]]) dp.poly([[dx - 9, yy - s * 14], [dx, yy], [dx + 9, yy - s * 14]], { p: pDim, k: `dimh${s}` });
      light.line(X(78) + 8, ya, dx + 12, ya, { p: pDim, k: 'ext1' });
      text(ctx, '+2.5 cm', dx + 22, (ya + yb) / 2 + 2, { size: 34, mono: true, reveal: seg(t, T.tCm, T.tCm + 0.3) });
      text(ctx, 'overnight', dx + 22, (ya + yb) / 2 + 42, { size: 30, style: 'italic', reveal: seg(t, T.tOver, T.tOver + 0.35) });
    }
    // the long flat run: a bracket under it, "63 days"
    const pBr = seg(t, T.t63, T.tFlat + 0.3);
    if (pBr > 0) {
      const y = Y(67.0) + 28, a = X(78) + 4, b = X(141);
      const bp = C.pen('bracket', { width: 2 });
      bp.poly([[a, y - 10], [a, y], [lerp(a, b, pBr), y]], { k: 'br', p: 1 });
      if (pBr >= 1) bp.line(b, y, b, y - 10, { k: 'brEnd' });
      text(ctx, '63 days', (a + b) / 2, y + 40, { size: 34, mono: true, align: 'center', reveal: seg(t, T.tDays, T.tDays + 0.4) });
    }

    // ---- optional: the front page and the big name ---------------------------------------------------
    if (isFinite(T.tFront)) drawFrontPage(ctx, C, t, T);

    // ---- the baby on the current step --------------------------------------------------------------------
    drawBaby(ctx, C, t, T, pos);
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

/** The baby: on the flat the pen is drawing, behind the tip; hops up each riser; grows per step. */
function drawBaby(ctx, C, t, T, pos) {
  const stand = (tt) => {
    const ps = posAt(tt, T.K);
    const i = Math.min(V.length - 2, Math.floor(ps));
    const flat = V[i][1] === V[i + 1][1];
    // the level it stands on: this flat, or the bottom of the riser being drawn
    const lvl = flat ? V[i][1] : V[i][1];
    const startX = X(V[i][0]);
    const tipX = X(vAt(ps)[0]);
    return { x: flat ? Math.max(startX + 46, tipX - 56) : startX - 56, lvl };
  };
  // smooth the hop: average the standing point over the last 0.22 s (pure: sampled at fixed offsets)
  let sx = 0, sl = 0;
  const n = 6;
  for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const s = stand(t - (k / (n - 1)) * 0.22); sx += s.x; sl += Y(s.lvl); }
  sx /= n; sl /= n;
  let steps = 0, hop = 0;
  for (const ri of RISERS) {
    // when the riser to vertex ri is complete
    let tr = Infinity;
    for (let k = 1; k < T.K.length; k++) if (T.K[k][1] >= ri && T.K[k - 1][1] < ri) { tr = lerp(T.K[k - 1][0], T.K[k][0], (ri - T.K[k - 1][1]) / (T.K[k][1] - T.K[k - 1][1])); break; }
    if (t >= tr) steps++;
    hop += Math.sin(Math.PI * seg(t, tr - 0.02, tr + 0.24)) * 34;
  }
  const r = 32 + 3 * steps;
  const wide = isFinite(T.tCm) && t >= T.tCm && t < T.tCm + 0.9;
  const glum = isFinite(T.tMocked) && t >= T.tMocked;
  const nope = isFinite(T.tNope) && t >= T.tNope && t < T.tNope + 0.5;
  const grin = isFinite(T.tArrive) && t >= T.tArrive - 0.3 && t < T.tArrive + 0.9;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= C.inP;
  C.baby({
    x: sx, y: babyStandY(sl, r) - hop, r,
    eyes: wide ? 'wide' : glum ? 'half' : 'open', open: blink(t, 53),
    look: glum ? [-0.8, -0.3] : [0.6, -0.5], mouth: glum ? 'press' : grin ? 'grin' : wide ? 'o' : 'smile', mouthOpen: 0.5,
    tilt: nope ? 0.12 * Math.sin((t - T.tNope) * 30) : 0.04 * Math.sin(t * 3.1),
    free: hop > 4 ? { mode: 'up', angle: t * 6 } : { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: hop > 4 ? 'up' : 'down', angle: t * 6 + 1 },
    cap: { to: [2.8, -1.8], sway: t },
  });
  ctx.restore();
}

/** The newspaper stamps in on "Front page"; a big name's bubble, struck through on "mocked her". */
function drawFrontPage(ctx, C, t, T) {
  const t0 = T.tFront;
  if (t < t0 - 0.1) return;
  const N = PAPER;
  const k = clamp((t - (t0 - 0.1)) / 0.1), after = t - t0;
  const fall = 1 + 0.3 * (1 - ease.inQuad(k));
  const squash = after >= 0 ? 1 - 0.08 * Math.exp(-after / 0.05) * Math.cos(after * 40) : 1;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= after >= 0 ? 1 : 0.35 + 0.65 * k;
  ctx.translate(N.x + N.w / 2, N.y + N.h / 2);
  ctx.rotate(-0.035);
  ctx.scale(fall * (2 - squash), fall * squash);
  ctx.translate(-N.w / 2, -N.h / 2);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, N.w, N.h);
  const pen = C.pen('paper', { width: 2.6 });
  pen.rect(0, 0, N.w, N.h, { k: 'edge' });
  text(ctx, 'The Daily Measure', N.w / 2, 46, { size: 36, weight: 700, align: 'center' });
  pen.line(14, 60, N.w - 14, 60, { k: 'r1', width: 2.4 });
  pen.line(14, 67, N.w - 14, 67, { k: 'r2', width: 1.2 });
  text(ctx, 'FRONT PAGE · 1992', N.w / 2, 92, { size: 22, mono: true, align: 'center', color: P.ink2 });
  text(ctx, 'BABIES GROW IN LEAPS', N.w / 2, 132, { size: 30, weight: 700, align: 'center' });
  const lp = C.pen('paper-lines', { width: 1.4, color: P.ink2 });
  for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) lp.line(18 + c * 106, 152 + j * 14, 18 + c * 106 + (j === 3 ? 60 : 92), 152 + j * 14, { k: `l${c},${j}` });
  ctx.restore();

  // the big name: a small ink adult and a speech bubble, struck through
  const tBig = first(T.tBig, t0 + 0.6);
  const pA = seg(t, tBig, tBig + 0.35);
  if (pA <= 0) return;
  const ax = 820, ag = 418;
  C.adult({ x: ax, y: ag, u: 11, pose: 'stand', eyes: 'open', mouth: 'o', breath: 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 9), lean: -0.06, arms: [0.1, 0.9], p: pA, k: 'bigname' });
  const tName = first(T.tName, tBig + 0.15);
  const pB = seg(t, tName, tName + 0.25);
  if (pB <= 0) return;
  const B = { x: 860, y: 132, w: 268, h: 70 };
  const bp = C.pen('bubble', { width: 2.4 });
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(pB * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(B.x, B.y, B.w, B.h, 22); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
  bp.rrect(B.x, B.y, B.w, B.h, 22, { p: pB, k: 'bubble' });
  bp.poly([[B.x + 40, B.y + B.h - 2], [ax + 18, ag - 96], [B.x + 74, B.y + B.h - 1]], { p: pB, k: 'tail' });
  const tM = first(T.tMocked, tName + 0.15);
  text(ctx, 'measurement error!', B.x + B.w / 2, B.y + 45, { size: 28, style: 'italic', align: 'center', reveal: seg(t, tM, tM + 0.2) });
  const tS = first(T.tHer, tM + 0.2);
  bp.line(B.x + 14, B.y + 42, B.x + B.w - 14, B.y + 30, { p: seg(t, tS, tS + 0.12), k: 'strike1', width: 3.2 });
  bp.line(B.x + 18, B.y + 26, B.x + B.w - 18, B.y + 46, { p: seg(t, tS + 0.06, tS + 0.18), k: 'strike2', width: 3.2 });
}
