file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/adolph.js
// The adjustable slope (Adolph et al., 2012).
//
// A walkway on a hinge at the floor: its top end carries the starting platform on a
// telescoping post, so raising the angle lifts the platform. A protractor at the hinge with a
// thick arc for the angle, a ratchet wheel turning with it, and a mono readout box top right
// (slope, then the counters). The slope sits flat until "raised", then ratchets up in 6° clicks
// spaced evenly from "raised" to "drop" (the line sings "six degrees" once, so the clicks are
// paced, not one per word): 14 clicks, 84° on "drop". The baby stands on the platform at the
// edge, peering over, wide-eyed at "sheer drop".
//
// Optional (the Grant line): an ink toddler walks along the platform at the shallow-slope
// pace, behind the baby, and goes straight off the top on "straight": a dotted trajectory, no
// impact, a "*" and the footnote "* caught by an experimenter".
// "Two thousand ...": steps roll to 2,368 /h; "seventeen falls" rolls to 17; "no stall" types
// "stalls 0". "Crawling isn't mandatory": the slope is let down to flat, a row of ink icons is
// drawn on the floor (crawl on "Crawling", bum-shuffle, bear-crawl, a "?" on "whatever") and the
// baby sits and bum-shuffles down the hall, happily, to a stop by "hall" (held there if the
// window runs on, as in the radio cut, where Hirsh-Pasek's slot is absorbed).

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, TAU } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, blink, BABY_GEOM } from '../../kit.js';

const FLOOR = 760;
const H = { x: 980, y: 740 };               // the hinge (walkway surface level)
const LEN = 440, BOARD = 20, PLAT = 380;    // walkway length, board thickness, platform length
const BOX = { x: 1330, y: 146, w: 420, h: 214 };
const ICONS = [{ x: 1262, name: 'crawl', word: 'crawling' }, { x: 1424, name: 'bum-shuffle', word: 'bum shuffle' }, { x: 1590, name: 'bear-crawl', word: 'bear crawl' }, { x: 1730, name: 'whatever', word: 'whatever' }];
const BR = 52;
const DEG = Math.PI / 180;

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

function plan(C) {
  const L = C.lines;
  const tRaise = first(C.wordTime('raised'), L[0] ? L[0].start + 0.6 : Infinity, C.start + 0.8);
  const tDrop = Math.max(tRaise + 0.8, first(C.wordTime('drop'), tRaise + 2));
  const lC = C.lineFor('crawling') || null;
  const tCrawl = first(C.wordTime('crawling'), lC ? lC.start : Infinity);
  const tMand = first(C.wordTime('mandatory'), tCrawl + 0.5);
  const tShuffle = first(C.wordTime('bum shuffle'), tMand + 0.7);
  const tHall = Math.max(tShuffle + 1, first(C.wordTime('hall'), tShuffle + 2.2));
  const lS = C.lineFor('steps an hour') || C.lineFor('seventeen falls') || null;
  const tTwo = first(C.wordTime('two', { line: 'steps' }), lS ? lS.start : Infinity);
  const tSteps = first(C.wordTime('steps'), tTwo + 1.4);
  const tSeventeen = first(C.wordTime('seventeen'), tSteps + 0.7);
  const tFalls = first(C.wordTime('falls', { line: 'seventeen' }), tSeventeen + 0.5);
  const tStall = first(C.wordTime('stall'), C.wordTime('no', { line: 'seventeen' }), tFalls + 0.6);
  const lG = C.lineFor('grant') || null;
  const grant = lG ? {
    t0: first(C.wordTime('grant'), lG.start),
    tEdge: first(C.wordTime('straight'), C.wordTime('went', { line: 'grant' }), lG.end - 0.6),
    tTop: first(C.wordTime('top', { line: 'grant' }), lG.end - 0.2),
  } : null;
  if (grant) grant.tTop = Math.max(grant.tTop, grant.tEdge + 0.35);
  return { tRaise, tDrop, tCrawl, tMand, tShuffle, tHall, tTwo, tSteps, tSeventeen, tFalls, tStall, grant,
    tSheer: first(C.wordTime('sheer'), tDrop - 0.3) };
}

/** The slope angle (degrees): 14 clicks of 6° from "raised", the last landing on "drop"; let down on "Crawling". */
function angleAt(t, T) {
  const n = 14, dt = (T.tDrop - T.tRaise) / (n - 0.6);
  let up;
  if (t <= T.tRaise) up = 0;
  else {
    const f = (t - T.tRaise) / dt;
    const i = Math.floor(f);
    up = i >= n ? n : i + ease.outBack(clamp((f - i) / 0.4), 2.4);
  }
  const down = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tCrawl, T.tMand));
  return 6 * Math.min(up, n) * (1 - down);
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();
    const T = plan(C);
    const th = angleAt(t, T) * DEG;
    const E = { x: H.x - LEN * Math.cos(th), y: H.y - LEN * Math.sin(th) };
    const pIn = C.inP;

    const light = C.pen('light', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 });
    light.line(170, FLOOR, 1790, FLOOR, { p: pIn, k: 'floor', width: 2 });

    // ---- the landing, the protractor, the walkway on its hinge -----------------------------------
    const pen = C.pen('rig', { width: 2.8 });
    pen.rect(H.x + 4, H.y, 210, FLOOR - H.y, { p: pIn, k: 'landing' });
    C.pen('landing-h', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([H.x + 4, H.y, 210, FLOOR - H.y], { gap: 8, p: pIn, k: 'landing-h' });
    drawProtractor(ctx, C, th, pIn);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(H.x, H.y); ctx.rotate(Math.PI + th);
    // local frame: +x up the walkway, -y below its surface
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(pIn * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(0, -BOARD, LEN, BOARD); ctx.restore();
    C.pen('walk-h', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([0, -BOARD, LEN, BOARD], { gap: 9, angle: 0.9, p: pIn, k: 'walk-h' });
    pen.rect(0, -BOARD, LEN, BOARD, { p: pIn, k: 'walkway' });
    ctx.restore();
    drawRatchet(ctx, C, th, pIn);

    // ---- the platform on its post, carried by the walkway's top end -------------------------------
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(E.x, E.y);
    const postH = FLOOR - (E.y + BOARD);
    if (postH > 2) {
      pen.line(-PLAT / 2 - 12, BOARD, -PLAT / 2 - 12, BOARD + postH, { k: 'postL', width: 2.4 });
      pen.line(-PLAT / 2 + 12, BOARD, -PLAT / 2 + 12, BOARD + postH, { k: 'postR', width: 2.4 });
      light.line(-PLAT / 2 - 40, BOARD + postH, -PLAT / 2 + 40, BOARD + postH, { k: 'postFoot', width: 2.4 });
    }
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(pIn * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(-PLAT, 0, PLAT, BOARD); ctx.restore();
    C.pen('plat-h', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([-PLAT, 0, PLAT, BOARD], { gap: 9, angle: -0.9, p: pIn, k: 'plat-h' });
    pen.rect(-PLAT, 0, PLAT, BOARD, { p: pIn, k: 'platform' });
    ctx.restore();

    // ---- the readout box ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    drawReadout(ctx, C, t, T, th, pIn);

    // ---- the icon row ("Crawling isn't mandatory") ----------------------------------------------------
    ICONS.forEach((ic, i) => {
      const t0 = first(C.wordTime(ic.word), i === 0 ? T.tCrawl : T.tShuffle + 0.5 * i);
      const p = seg(t, t0, t0 + 0.4);
      if (p > 0) drawIcon(ctx, C, ic, p, i, t);
    });

    // ---- Grant (optional) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (T.grant) drawGrant(ctx, C, t, T.grant, E);

    // ---- the baby ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    drawTheBaby(ctx, C, t, T, th, E);
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

function drawProtractor(ctx, C, th, p) {
  const R = 150;
  const pr = C.pen('protractor', { width: 2, color: P.ink2 });
  pr.arc(H.x, H.y, R, Math.PI, Math.PI * 1.5, { p, k: 'arc' });
  for (let d = 0; d <= 90; d += 10) {
    const a = Math.PI + d * DEG, l = d % 30 === 0 ? 18 : 10;
    pr.line(H.x + Math.cos(a) * R, H.y + Math.sin(a) * R, H.x + Math.cos(a) * (R - l), H.y + Math.sin(a) * (R - l), { p, k: `tk${d}`, width: 1.6 });
  }
  text(ctx, '0°', H.x - R - 14, H.y - 8, { size: 26, mono: true, color: P.ink2, align: 'right', alpha: p });
  text(ctx, '90°', H.x + 8, H.y - R - 6, { size: 26, mono: true, color: P.ink2, alpha: p });
  // the angle: a thick ink arc from the floor to the walkway
  if (th > 0.01) {
    const pts = [];
    const n = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(th / (2 * DEG)));
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const a = Math.PI + (th * i) / n; pts.push([H.x + Math.cos(a) * 92, H.y + Math.sin(a) * 92]); }
    C.pen('angle', { width: 3.2 }).poly(pts, { k: 'theta' });
  }
}

function drawRatchet(ctx, C, th, p) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(H.x, H.y); ctx.rotate(-th * 2);
  const g = C.pen('ratchet', { width: 2.2 });
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 22, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
  g.circle(0, 0, 20, { p, k: 'wheel' });
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const a = (i / 12) * TAU, a2 = a + 0.3;
    g.poly([[Math.cos(a) * 20, Math.sin(a) * 20], [Math.cos(a) * 28, Math.sin(a) * 28], [Math.cos(a2) * 20, Math.sin(a2) * 20]], { p, k: `tooth${i}`, width: 1.6 });
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink; ctx.globalAlpha *= p;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  // the pawl, fixed
  C.pen('pawl', { width: 2.4 }).poly([[H.x + 52, H.y - 4], [H.x + 30, H.y - 22], [H.x + 26, H.y - 14]], { p, k: 'pawl' });
}

function drawReadout(ctx, C, t, T, th, p) {
  const B = BOX;
  C.pen('box', { width: 2.4 }).rect(B.x, B.y, B.w, B.h, { p, k: 'box' });
  const rowY = [B.y + 58, B.y + 120, B.y + 182];
  const lab = (s, i, a) => text(ctx, s, B.x + 22, rowY[i], { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: a });
  const val = (s, i, a) => text(ctx, s, B.x + B.w - 22, rowY[i] + 2, { size: 44, mono: true, align: 'right', alpha: a });
  lab('slope', 0, seg(p, 0.4, 1));
  val(`${Math.round(th / DEG)}°`, 0, seg(p, 0.5, 1));
  // the counters, rolling on their words
  if (isFinite(T.tTwo)) {
    const a = seg(t, T.tTwo - 0.1, T.tTwo + 0.2);
    lab('steps', 1, a);
    const n = Math.round(2368 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tTwo, T.tSteps)));
    if (a > 0) val(`${n.toLocaleString('en-US')} /h`, 1, a);
    const b = seg(t, T.tSeventeen - 0.1, T.tSeventeen + 0.2);
    lab('falls', 2, b);
    const f = Math.round(17 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tSeventeen, T.tFalls + 0.2)));
    if (b > 0) val(String(f), 2, b);
    text(ctx, 'stalls 0', B.x + B.w - 22, B.y + B.h + 40, { size: 28, mono: true, align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, T.tStall, T.tStall + 0.3) });
  }
}

/** Small side-view ink icons on the floor; the bum-shuffle slot is the baby's (an empty ring under it). */
function drawIcon(ctx, C, ic, p, i, t) {
  const pen = C.pen(`icon-${ic.name}`, { width: 2.4 });
  const x = ic.x, y = FLOOR;
  text(ctx, ic.name, x, y + 42, { size: 26, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.4, 1) });
  const body = (cx, cy, w, h, rot) => {
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(cx, cy); ctx.rotate(rot);
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 10); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
    pen.rrect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 10, { p, k: 'body' });
    ctx.fillStyle = P.ink; ctx.globalAlpha *= p;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(w / 2 - 16, -h / 2 + 8, 6, 11, 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  };
  if (ic.name === 'crawl') {
    pen.line(x + 22, y - 30, x + 26, y, { p, k: 'arm' });
    pen.poly([[x - 24, y - 30], [x - 24, y - 2], [x - 52, y - 2]], { p, k: 'leg' });
    body(x, y - 44, 76, 40, 0.05);
  } else if (ic.name === 'bear-crawl') {
    pen.line(x + 22, y - 36, x + 30, y, { p, k: 'arm' });
    pen.line(x - 22, y - 54, x - 30, y, { p, k: 'leg' });
    body(x, y - 62, 76, 38, -0.32);
  } else if (ic.name === 'bum-shuffle') {
    // the baby's own slot: a light dashed ring on the floor where it sits
    C.pen('slot', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 }).dashed(Array.from({ length: 41 }, (_, k) => [x + Math.cos((k / 40) * TAU) * 58, y - 6 + Math.sin((k / 40) * TAU) * 10]), { p, k: 'ring', dash: 8, gap: 7 });
  } else {
    C.pen('whatever', { width: 1.8, color: P.ink2 }).dashed([[x - 34, y - 2], [x - 34, y - 64], [x + 34, y - 64], [x + 34, y - 2]], { p, k: 'box', dash: 9, gap: 7 });
    text(ctx, '?', x, y - 18, { size: 46, style: 'italic', weight: 700, align: 'center', alpha: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
  }
}

/** Grant: an ink toddler at the shallow pace, behind the baby, straight off the top. */
function drawGrant(ctx, C, t, G, E) {
  if (t < G.t0) return;
  const r = 42;
  const xs = E.x - PLAT + 40, walk = seg(t, G.t0, G.tEdge);
  const fall = seg(t, G.tEdge, G.tTop + 0.3);
  // the trajectory, in page coordinates from the edge (the slope is steady while he walks)
  const traj = (k) => [E.x + 8 + 230 * k, E.y - 52 * k + 440 * k * k];
  if (fall > 0) {
    const pts = Array.from({ length: 31 }, (_, i) => traj((i / 30) * 0.86));
    C.pen('grant-traj', { color: P.ink2 }).dotted(pts, { p: clamp(fall / 0.86), k: 'traj', gap: 13, r: 2.4 });
    if (fall >= 0.86) text(ctx, '*', traj(0.86)[0] + 20, traj(0.86)[1] + 10, { size: 46, weight: 700 });
    text(ctx, '* caught by an experimenter', 190, 812, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, G.tTop, G.tTop + 0.5) });
  }
  let x, y, tilt, a = 1;
  if (fall <= 0) {
    x = lerp(xs, E.x - 34, walk);
    y = E.y - 1.9 * r - 6 - 5 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 9));
    tilt = 0.08 * Math.sin(t * 9);
  } else {
    const [px, py] = traj(Math.min(fall, 0.86));
    x = px; y = py - 1.9 * r; tilt = 0.4 + fall * 1.6;
    a = 1 - seg(fall, 0.55, 0.86);
  }
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  C.inkBaby({ x, y, r, k: 'grant', eyes: 'open', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.3, look: [0.9, 0.2], tilt, free: { mode: fall > 0 ? 'up' : 'down', angle: t * 6 }, other: { mode: fall > 0 ? 'up' : 'down', angle: t * 6 + 2 }, cap: null });
  ctx.restore();
}

/** The baby: at the platform edge peering over; follows Grant; reads the counters; bum-shuffles away. */
function drawTheBaby(ctx, C, t, T, th, E) {
  const sit = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tShuffle - 0.25, T.tShuffle));
  const x0 = E.x - 70;
  // the shuffle: eight scoots from "Bum-shuffle" to "hall", then it stays put
  const sk = seg(t, T.tShuffle, T.tHall), n = 8;
  const si = Math.min(n - 1, Math.floor(sk * n)), sf = sk * n - si;
  const scoot = sk >= 1 ? 1 : (si + ease.inOutQuad(sf)) / n;
  const shuffleX = lerp(x0, ICONS[1].x, scoot);
  const hopping = sk > 0 && sk < 1;
  const hopY = hopping ? -10 * Math.sin(Math.PI * sf) : 0;
  const x = sit > 0 ? (sk > 0 ? shuffleX : x0) : x0;
  const groundY = E.y;
  const steep = clamp((th / DEG - 30) / 40);
  const sheer = t >= T.tSheer && t < T.tCrawl;
  // where it looks: down the slope, at Grant, up at the counters, ahead while shuffling
  let look = [0.7 * (1 - steep) + 0.5 * steep, 0.3 + 0.6 * steep];
  if (T.grant && t >= T.grant.t0 && t < T.grant.tTop + 0.8) {
    const w = seg(t, T.grant.t0, T.grant.tEdge);
    look = t < T.grant.tEdge ? [lerp(-1, 0.9, w), 0.1] : [0.8, 0.9];
  }
  if (isFinite(T.tTwo) && t >= T.tTwo && t < T.tCrawl) look = [0.9, -0.7];
  if (sit > 0) look = [0.8, 0.1];
  const wince = isFinite(T.tSeventeen) && t >= T.tFalls && t < T.tFalls + 0.4;
  const happy = sit > 0.5 || (isFinite(T.tStall) && t >= T.tStall && t < T.tCrawl);
  const face = {
    eyes: wince ? 'squeeze' : sheer || steep > 0.8 ? 'wide' : 'open', open: blink(t, 61), look,
    mouth: happy ? 'grin' : steep > 0.5 ? 'o' : 'smile', mouthOpen: happy ? 0.5 : 0.35, brows: sheer && !happy ? 'up' : null,
  };
  const r = BR;
  const standY = groundY - (BABY_GEOM.body.b + BABY_GEOM.legH) * r;
  const sitY = groundY - (BABY_GEOM.body.b + 0.02) * r;
  const y = lerp(standY, sitY, sit) + hopY;
  const tilt = sit > 0 ? (hopping ? 0.1 * Math.sin(sf * TAU) : 0) : 0.12 * steep;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= C.inP;
  // motion marks behind the shuffling baby
  if (hopping) {
    const mp = C.pen('marks', { width: 1.8, color: P.ink2 });
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) mp.line(x - 1.6 * r - 14, y + (0.6 + k * 0.4) * r, x - 1.6 * r - 54 + k * 8, y + (0.6 + k * 0.4) * r, { k: `m${k}` });
  }
  babyWithLegs(ctx, C, {
    x, y, r, tilt, ...face,
    free: sit > 0.5 ? { mode: 'wave', angle: t * 7 } : steep > 0.6 ? { mode: 'hold', at: [1.5, 1.3] } : { mode: 'down' },
    other: sit > 0.5 ? { mode: 'up', angle: t * 7 + 1 } : { mode: 'down' },
    cap: { to: [-2.6, -2.2], sway: t },
  }, (i) => ({ a: 0, len: lerp(1, 0, sit) }));
  // sitting: the feet stick out toward us at the front of the body
  if (sit > 0) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= sit;
    ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(tilt);
    ctx.fillStyle = P.skinShade; ctx.strokeStyle = P.ink; ctx.lineWidth = r * 0.032;
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(k * 0.55 * r, 1.58 * r, 0.24 * r, 0.16 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// ---- the baby with posable legs (as in barburoth.js; kept local to this lab) ----------------

function bodyPath(ctx, r) {
  const { l, r: rt, t, b, rad, bulge } = BABY_GEOM.body;
  const L = l * r, R = rt * r, T = t * r, B = b * r, k = rad * r, q = bulge * r;
  ctx.moveTo(L + k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, T - q, R - k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, T, R, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R + q, (T + B) / 2, R, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, B, R - k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, B + q, L + k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, B, L, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L - q, (T + B) / 2, L, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, T, L + k, T);
  ctx.closePath();
}

/** The clay baby with legs posed by legs(i) -> { a, len }; its own legs are clipped away. */
function babyWithLegs(ctx, C, o, legs) {
  const r = o.r, G = BABY_GEOM;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(o.x, o.y); ctx.rotate(o.tilt || 0);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.skinShade; ctx.strokeStyle = P.ink; ctx.lineWidth = r * 0.034 * 0.9; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const { a, len } = legs(i);
    if (len <= 0.02) continue;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(G.legs[i] * r, (G.body.b - 0.14) * r);
    ctx.rotate(a);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-(G.legW / 2) * r, 0, G.legW * r, (0.14 + G.legH + 0.02) * r * len + 0.06 * r * len, 0.08 * r);
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.rect(-8 * r, -10 * r, 16 * r, 11.15 * r);
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.rotate(-(o.tilt || 0)); ctx.translate(-o.x, -o.y);
  ctx.clip();
  C.baby({ ...o });
  ctx.restore();
}

Writesongs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/hirshpasek.js
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/hirshpasek.js
// Clauses are perceptual units (Hirsh-Pasek et al., 1987).
//
// Two loudspeakers, left and right, each with its version of a Cinderella sentence as an ink
// waveform envelope (outline, light fill strokes), two clauses bracketed under it. The left
// one draws on "Hirsh-Pasek" and splits on "splits": the pause opens at the clause boundary;
// the right one draws on "Cinderella" and splits on "pauses", in the middle of the first
// clause. A tick lands on the left pause on "pauses", a cross on the right one on "middle";
// "clause" labels the brackets. Each speaker sends light sound arcs while its waveform plays.
// The baby stands between them watching each in turn; on "turn" its head turns to the
// natural one (left), whose speaker keeps breathing; mouth pressed on "Can't talk", a grin on
// "know the laws".
//
// Absent from the radio cut: with no lines the lab has no window and this never runs; it also
// returns at once if it is ever called without lines.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, hash, TAU } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, babyStandY, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const CY = 330;                                   // waveform centre line
const WF = [
  { x: 350, w: 560, split: 0.56, mark: 'tick', title: 'pause at the clause boundary' },
  { x: 1010, w: 560, split: 0.27, mark: 'cross', title: 'pause mid-clause' },
];
const GAP = 52, AMP = 66, NSYL = 15;
const SPK = [{ x: 220, dir: 1 }, { x: 1700, dir: -1 }];
const CLAUSE = 0.56;                               // the clause boundary, in both versions
const BABY = { x: 960, ground: 800, r: 72 };

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

/** The speech envelope at u (0..1): syllable bumps, silent at the split. Same sentence for both. */
function env(u, split) {
  let a = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < NSYL; j++) {
    const c = (j + 0.5) / NSYL, h = 0.45 + 0.55 * hash(j, 311);
    a += h * Math.exp(-(((u - c) / 0.028) ** 2));
  }
  const edge = clamp(u / 0.02) * clamp((1 - u) / 0.02);
  const hole = clamp(Math.abs(u - split) / 0.025);
  return Math.min(1, a) * edge * hole * hole;
}

function plan(C) {
  const L = C.lines;
  const t0 = first(C.wordTime('hirsh pasek'), C.wordTime('hirsh'), L[0] ? L[0].start : Infinity, C.start + 0.2);
  const tSplit = first(C.wordTime('splits'), t0 + 0.55);
  const tCin = first(C.wordTime('cinderella'), tSplit + 0.4);
  const tPauses = first(C.wordTime('pauses'), tCin + 0.6);
  const tMiddle = first(C.wordTime('middle'), tPauses + 0.6);
  const tClause = first(C.wordTime('clause'), tMiddle + 0.6);
  const tTurn = first(C.wordTime('turn'), L[1] ? L[1].start + 0.1 : Infinity, tClause + 0.4);
  return {
    draw: [[t0 + 0.05, tSplit + 0.05], [tCin, tPauses - 0.02]],
    split: [[tSplit, tSplit + 0.3], [tPauses, tPauses + 0.3]],
    mark: [tPauses, tMiddle], tClause, tTurn,
    tBreathes: first(C.wordTime('breathes'), tTurn + 0.6),
    tCant: first(C.wordTime('cant'), tTurn + 0.9), tKnow: first(C.wordTime('know'), tTurn + 1.5),
  };
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();
    const T = plan(C);

    // ---- the speakers ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    SPK.forEach((s, i) => {
      drawSpeaker(ctx, C, s, i, C.inP);
      // sound arcs: while the speaker's waveform plays, and the natural one again after "turn"
      const [a, b] = T.draw[i];
      const on = clamp(Math.min(seg(t, a, a + 0.1), 1 - seg(t, b, b + 0.3))) + (i === 0 ? seg(t, T.tTurn, T.tTurn + 0.3) * 0.8 : 0);
      if (on > 0.01) drawWaves(ctx, C, s, i, t, clamp(on));
    });

    // ---- the two waveforms ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    WF.forEach((wf, i) => drawWave(ctx, C, wf, i, t, T));

    // ---- the baby ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const turn = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tTurn, T.tTurn + 0.35));
    const watch = t < T.draw[1][0] ? -0.8 : t < T.tTurn ? 0.8 : 0.8;
    const lookX = lerp(watch, -1, turn);
    const cant = t >= T.tCant && t < T.tKnow;
    const know = t >= T.tKnow;
    const r = BABY.r;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= C.inP;
    C.pen('floor', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 }).line(BABY.x - 130, BABY.ground + 2, BABY.x + 130, BABY.ground + 2, { p: C.inP, k: 'floor' });
    C.baby({
      x: BABY.x, y: babyStandY(BABY.ground, r), r,
      eyes: t >= T.tBreathes && !know ? 'focus' : 'open', open: blink(t, 71), look: [lookX, -0.75],
      mouth: know ? 'grin' : cant ? 'press' : 'o', mouthOpen: know ? 0.5 : 0.25,
      tilt: lerp(0.07 * Math.sign(watch), -0.16, turn),
      free: know ? { mode: 'up', angle: t * 5 } : { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' },
      cap: { to: [2.6, -1.6], sway: t },
    });
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

function drawSpeaker(ctx, C, s, i, p) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(s.x, CY); ctx.scale(s.dir, 1);
  const pen = C.pen(`speaker${i}`, { width: 2.6 });
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -36); ctx.lineTo(46, -74); ctx.lineTo(46, 74); ctx.lineTo(0, 36); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  C.pen(`magnet${i}`, { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([-34, -30, 34, 60], { gap: 7, p, k: 'mag' });
  pen.rect(-34, -30, 34, 60, { p, k: 'box' });
  pen.poly([[0, -36], [46, -74], [46, 74], [0, 36]], { p, k: 'cone', close: true });
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawWaves(ctx, C, s, i, t, a) {
  const pen = C.pen(`waves${i}`, { width: 2, color: P.ink2 });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(s.x + s.dir * 62, CY);
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
    const ph = ((t * 1.4 + k / 3) % 1);
    const R = 16 + ph * 70;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= a * (1 - ph);
    const a0 = s.dir > 0 ? -0.6 : Math.PI - 0.6, a1 = a0 + 1.2;
    pen.arc(0, 0, R, a0, a1, { k: `w${k}` });
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

/** One version of the sentence: envelope outline and fill strokes, split open at its pause. */
function drawWave(ctx, C, wf, i, t, T) {
  const [ta, tb] = T.draw[i];
  const pd = seg(t, ta, tb);
  if (pd <= 0) return;
  const k = ease.outBack(seg(t, T.split[i][0], T.split[i][1]), 1.6);
  const W = wf.w - GAP;                            // the sentence's width before the split
  const N = 90;
  const fillPen = C.pen(`fill${i}`, { width: 1.3, color: P.ink2 });
  const outPen = C.pen(`env${i}`, { width: 2.6 });
  // light centre line
  C.pen(`axis${i}`, { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).line(wf.x - 10, CY, wf.x + wf.w + 10, CY, { p: pd, k: 'axis', alpha: 0.7 });
  text(ctx, wf.title, wf.x + wf.w / 2, CY - 118, { size: 28, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, align: 'center', reveal: seg(pd, 0.3, 1) });
  for (const half of [0, 1]) {
    const u0 = half ? wf.split : 0, u1 = half ? 1 : wf.split;
    ctx.save();
    if (half) ctx.translate(GAP * k, 0);
    // fill strokes, every 7 px, as the pen reaches them
    for (let x = Math.ceil(u0 * W / 7) * 7; x <= u1 * W; x += 7) {
      const u = x / W;
      if (u > pd) break;
      const h = env(u, wf.split) * AMP * (0.55 + 0.45 * hash(Math.round(x), 401 + i));
      if (h < 1.5) continue;
      fillPen.line(wf.x + x, CY - h, wf.x + x, CY + h, { k: `f${Math.round(x)}`, wobble: 0.3 });
    }
    const up = [], dn = [];
    for (let j = 0; j <= N; j++) {
      const u = lerp(u0, u1, j / N), h = env(u, wf.split) * AMP;
      up.push([wf.x + u * W, CY - h]); dn.push([wf.x + u * W, CY + h]);
    }
    const ph = clamp((pd - u0) / (u1 - u0));
    outPen.poly(up, { p: ph, k: `up${half}` });
    outPen.poly(dn, { p: ph, k: `dn${half}` });
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // the clause brackets (the boundary moves with the split when it is the pause)
  const bx = (u) => wf.x + u * W + (u > wf.split ? GAP * k : 0);
  const by = CY + AMP + 26;
  const bp = C.pen(`brackets${i}`, { width: 1.8, color: P.ink2 });
  const tc = T.tClause;
  [[0, CLAUSE - 0.015], [CLAUSE + 0.015, 1]].forEach(([a, b], j) => {
    const pts = [[bx(a), by - 10], [bx(a), by], [bx(b), by], [bx(b), by - 10]];
    bp.poly(pts, { p: seg(pd, 0.2 + j * 0.4, 0.6 + j * 0.4), k: `br${j}` });
    text(ctx, 'clause', (bx(a) + bx(b)) / 2, by + 36, { size: 28, style: 'italic', align: 'center', reveal: seg(t, tc + j * 0.1, tc + 0.3 + j * 0.1) });
  });
  // the mark over the pause
  const tm = T.mark[i];
  const pm = seg(t, tm, tm + 0.25);
  if (pm > 0) {
    const mx = wf.x + wf.split * W + GAP * k / 2, my = CY - AMP - 34;
    const mp = C.pen(`mark${i}`, { width: 3.4 });
    if (wf.mark === 'tick') mp.poly([[mx - 16, my], [mx - 4, my + 14], [mx + 20, my - 18]], { p: pm, k: 'tick' });
    else { mp.line(mx - 15, my - 15, mx + 15, my + 15, { p: seg(pm, 0, 0.5), k: 'x1' }); mp.line(mx + 15, my - 15, mx - 15, my + 15, { p: seg(pm, 0.5, 1), k: 'x2' }); }
  }
}

Writesongs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/saffran.js1:14 PM

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/saffran.js
// Statistical learning of word boundaries (Saffran et al., 1996).
//
// A loudspeaker on the right feeds a stream of syllables in mono that scrolls left over the
// figure: four three-syllable nonsense words in a seeded order with no word twice in a row,
// starting "pa bee coo go la too" as it is sung. Arcs over each pair of neighbours carry the
// transitional probability: all light at first; on "one" the arcs inside words go full ink and
// read 1.0, on "three" the arcs across word edges go light dashed and read .33. A readout top
// right rolls to 2:00 on "two minutes" and to 45 reps on "forty-five reps each". The stream
// eases to a stop on "I'm", the word boundaries cut in as vertical lines, and on "carving
// speech" the four words lift out of the stream and stack, bracketed. The baby (8 months)
// listens from the lower left, concentrating on the probabilities, delighted on "carving".

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, hash } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { P, text, babyStandY, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const WORDS = [['pa', 'bee', 'coo'], ['go', 'la', 'too'], ['ti', 'bu', 'do'], ['da', 'ro', 'pi']];
const SW = 132, BASE = 452, X0 = 300, V = 150, SIZE = 50;
const XL = 200, XR = 1610;                    // the visible stream
const ARC = { lift: 52, h: 38 };
const SPK = { x: 1700, y: BASE - 16 };
const BOX = { x: 1300, y: 140, w: 440, h: 150 };
const STACK = { x: 1000, y: 600, dy: 62 };
const BABY = { x: 330, ground: 806, r: 82 };

// word order: pabeecoo, golatoo, then seeded, never the same word twice in a row
const SEQ = (() => { const s = [0, 1]; for (let k = 2; k < 64; k++) s.push((s[k - 1] + 1 + Math.floor(hash(k, 77) * 3)) % 4); return s; })();
const syl = (n) => WORDS[SEQ[Math.floor(n / 3)]][n % 3];

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

function plan(C) {
  const L = C.lines;
  const tStop = first(C.wordTime('im', { line: 'carving' }), C.wordTime('carving') - 0.12, C.end - 0.5);
  return {
    tName: first(C.wordTime('saffran'), C.start + 0.2),
    tTwo: first(C.wordTime('two', { line: 'minutes' }), L[0] ? L[0].start + 1.2 : Infinity),
    tFortyFive: first(C.wordTime('forty five'), C.wordTime('forty'), L[0] ? L[0].start + 1.6 : Infinity),
    tEach: first(C.wordTime('each'), C.wordTime('reps') + 0.3, L[0] ? L[0].end - 0.2 : Infinity),
    tOne: first(C.wordTime('one', { line: 'probability' }), L[1] ? L[1].start + 0.4 : Infinity, tStop - 1.2),
    tThree: first(C.wordTime('three', { line: 'probability' }), L[1] ? L[1].start + 1.2 : Infinity, tStop - 0.5),
    tStop, tCarve: Math.max(tStop + 0.1, first(C.wordTime('carving'), tStop + 0.12)),
  };
}

/** How far the stream has scrolled: steady, easing to a stop at tStop (pure). */
function scrollAt(t, s, tStop) {
  const d = 0.3, a = tStop - d;
  const k = seg(t, a, tStop);
  return V * (Math.min(t, a) - s) + (V * d / 2) * (2 * k - k * k);
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();
    const T = plan(C);
    const sc = scrollAt(t, C.start, T.tStop);
    const xOf = (n) => X0 + n * SW - sc;
    const revealX = lerp(XL, XR + 60, C.inP);
    const nLo = Math.max(0, Math.floor((XL - 80 - X0 + sc) / SW)), nHi = Math.ceil((XR + 80 - X0 + sc) / SW);
    const fade = (x) => clamp((x - XL) / 90) * clamp((XR - x) / 90) * clamp((revealX - x) / 60);
    const kIn = seg(t, T.tOne, T.tOne + 0.3), kX = seg(t, T.tThree, T.tThree + 0.3);

    drawSpeaker(ctx, C, C.inP, t);

    // ---- arcs (keyed by stream index, so each keeps its wobble while it scrolls) ---------------
    for (let n = nLo; n < nHi; n++) {
      const x = xOf(n), a = Math.min(fade(x), fade(x + SW));
      if (a <= 0) continue;
      const within = n % 3 !== 2;
      const pts = Array.from({ length: 17 }, (_, i) => { const u = i / 16; return [SW * u, -ARC.h * Math.sin(Math.PI * u)]; });
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(x, BASE - ARC.lift);
      ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
      if (within) {
        C.pen(`arc${n}`, { width: lerp(1.6, 3, kIn), color: mix(P.ink2, P.ink, kIn) }).poly(pts, { k: 'a' });
        text(ctx, '1.0', SW / 2, -ARC.h - 12, { size: 28, mono: true, align: 'center', reveal: kIn });
      } else {
        ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - kX; C.pen(`arc${n}`, { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 }).poly(pts, { k: 'a' }); ctx.restore();
        if (kX > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= kX; C.pen(`arcd${n}`, { width: 2, color: P.ink2 }).dashed(pts, { k: 'd', dash: 8, gap: 7 }); ctx.restore(); }
        text(ctx, '.33', SW / 2, -ARC.h - 12, { size: 28, mono: true, align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: kX });
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // ---- the syllables --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    for (let n = nLo; n < nHi; n++) {
      const x = xOf(n), a = fade(x);
      if (a <= 0) continue;
      text(ctx, syl(n), x, BASE, { size: SIZE, mono: true, align: 'center', alpha: a });
    }
    // ---- the boundaries cut in ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (t >= T.tStop) {
      const cp = C.pen('cuts', { width: 3.2 });
      for (let n = nLo; n < nHi; n++) {
        if (n % 3 !== 2) continue;
        const x = xOf(n) + SW / 2;
        if (x < XL + 30 || x > XR - 30) continue;
        const d = ((x - XL) / (XR - XL)) * 0.08;
        cp.line(x, BASE - ARC.lift - ARC.h - 50, x, BASE + 36, { p: seg(t, T.tStop + d, T.tStop + d + 0.1), k: `cut${n}` });
      }
    }
    // ---- the words stack ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const pS = seg(t, T.tCarve, T.tCarve + 0.08);
    if (pS > 0) {
      // the first whole instance of each word in view (the stream has stopped, so these are fixed)
      const seen = [];
      for (let k = Math.floor(nLo / 3); k * 3 < nHi && seen.length < 4; k++) {
        const x0 = xOf(k * 3), x2 = xOf(k * 3 + 2);
        if (x0 < XL + 40 || x2 > XR - 40 || seen.some((s) => s.w === SEQ[k])) continue;
        seen.push({ w: SEQ[k], x: (x0 + x2) / 2 });
      }
      const sp = C.pen('stack', { width: 2.2, color: P.ink2 });
      seen.forEach((s, j) => {
        const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tCarve + j * 0.05, T.tCarve + j * 0.05 + 0.2));
        if (k <= 0) return;
        const x = lerp(s.x, STACK.x, k), y = lerp(BASE, STACK.y + j * STACK.dy, k);
        text(ctx, WORDS[s.w].join(''), x, y, { size: 46, mono: true, weight: 700, align: k < 1 ? 'center' : 'center', alpha: clamp(k * 3) });
      });
      const bp = seg(t, T.tCarve + 0.15, T.tCarve + 0.32);
      const bx = STACK.x - 140, y0 = STACK.y - 44, y1 = STACK.y + (seen.length - 1) * STACK.dy + 14;
      sp.poly([[bx + 14, y0], [bx, y0], [bx, y1], [bx + 14, y1]], { p: bp, k: 'bracket' });
      text(ctx, 'words', bx - 16, (y0 + y1) / 2 + 10, { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, align: 'right', reveal: bp });
    }

    // ---- the readout ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const B = BOX;
    C.pen('box', { width: 2.4 }).rect(B.x, B.y, B.w, B.h, { p: C.inP, k: 'box' });
    text(ctx, 'exposure', B.x + 22, B.y + 56, { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(C.inP, 0.4, 1) });
    text(ctx, 'reps / word', B.x + 22, B.y + 118, { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(C.inP, 0.4, 1) });
    const secs = Math.floor(120 * ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, T.tTwo, T.tTwo + 0.5)) + 1e-6);
    text(ctx, `${Math.floor(secs / 60)}:${String(secs % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`, B.x + B.w - 22, B.y + 58, { size: 44, mono: true, align: 'right', alpha: C.inP });
    text(ctx, String(Math.round(45 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.tFortyFive, T.tEach + 0.15)))), B.x + B.w - 22, B.y + 120, { size: 44, mono: true, align: 'right', alpha: C.inP });

    // ---- the baby -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const r = BABY.r;
    const think = t >= T.tOne && t < T.tCarve;
    const joy = t >= T.tCarve;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= C.inP;
    C.pen('floor', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 }).line(BABY.x - 130, BABY.ground + 2, BABY.x + 130, BABY.ground + 2, { p: C.inP, k: 'floor' });
    C.baby({
      x: BABY.x, y: babyStandY(BABY.ground, r), r,
      eyes: joy ? 'wide' : think ? 'focus' : 'open', open: blink(t, 83), look: [0.75, -0.8],
      mouth: joy ? 'grin' : think ? 'press' : 'o', mouthOpen: joy ? 0.55 : 0.25,
      tilt: 0.06 * Math.sin(t * 2.4),
      free: joy ? { mode: 'up', angle: t * 6 } : { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' },
      cap: { to: [-1.4, 1.4], sway: t },
    });
    ctx.restore();
    text(ctx, 'age 8 mo', BABY.x + 1.6 * r + 20, BABY.ground - 10, { size: 28, mono: true, color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, T.tName, T.tName + 0.35) });
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

function drawSpeaker(ctx, C, p, t) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(SPK.x, SPK.y); ctx.scale(-1, 1);
  const pen = C.pen('speaker', { width: 2.6 });
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -32); ctx.lineTo(42, -66); ctx.lineTo(42, 66); ctx.lineTo(0, 32); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  C.pen('magnet', { width: 1.2, color: P.ink2 }).hatch([-30, -26, 30, 52], { gap: 7, p, k: 'mag' });
  pen.rect(-30, -26, 30, 52, { p, k: 'box' });
  pen.poly([[0, -32], [42, -66], [42, 66], [0, 32]], { p, k: 'cone', close: true });
  ctx.restore();
}

BashRender two 9:16 stills of my figures1:24 PM
input

All eight lab figures are finished and committed, and they hold up on both the keeper timing and the radio-cut simulation. --check passes on both data dirs (determinism and loop), and the smoke test passes for all eight ?plate= keys plus a 9:16 and a radio-sim query. I also rendered 22 stills across my labs in order and again reversed: they are byte-identical. No headless Chrome is left running. I only staged and committed my eight files, by explicit path; the other agents' work is untouched.

Draft: /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/renders/draft-labs-v1v2a-540p.mp4. It covers song time 14–114 s at 540p, with the audio offset to match (render.mjs passes -ss with --from). It is gitignored and not committed. It took 657 s with 9 workers, probably slowed by the other agents' renders: timed as stills, my labs cost no more per frame than the spine's chorus plate.

Per figure (frame times checked, in song seconds; radio-sim times where they matter):

  • Feldman (Fig. 1): the mothers' and fathers' curves plot together, and on "identical" the fathers' curve snaps onto the mothers', leaving a fading dotted ghost of where it was. The swab at the origin is drawn on "swabs" and drips on "spit", the key reads "n = 80 couples", and "n.s." brackets the two labels. Checked at 15.3, 18.5, 20.3, 22.25.
  • Abraham (Fig. 2): a side-view brain outline whose amygdala hatches in on "amygdala" and cross-hatches with short rays on "lit", plus "n = 48 fathers". This departs from your brief of one small, simple figure: the brain is large, and I added a screen showing the baby (the stimulus) with a dotted arrow to the brain, so the baby is still the data point. Say if you want it cut back. Checked at 22.9, 23.6, 24.95.
  • Tronick (Fig. 3): three panels, a play / b still face / c reunion. The adult goes flat on "froze", and the bids land on their words: smile, a reaching bid, fuss, cry with tears, hands to the mouth. The stopwatch is compressed: it runs from "froze" and reads 2:00 when panel c starts. A "repair" arrow runs from c back to a. Earlier panels' babies turn into the kit's ink copies. Checked at 26.5, 30.5, 36.8, 37.9, 39.6, 44.0.
  • Barbu-Roth (Fig. 6): the harnessed baby hangs over a perspective floor whose dots stream from "Dots"; the legs step on "swim", the astronaut inset arrives on "astronaut", and a "quadruped" leader labels the legs. On "skateboard" the harness lifts away and the baby lies on a skateboard on a plan-view mattress. I made it one continuous crossing rather than back-and-forth: the tally counts crossings on "crossed", "Twice" and "Three", and the eyes close on "rest". Checked at 66.4, 69.2, 71.8, 73.4, 75.3, 77.1.
  • Lampl (Fig. 7): a growth staircase against the light smooth curve. The notebook row is typed as its four points plot. It sits top-left with a dotted leader rather than literally above the step, because that space belongs to the staircase and the baby. The steps land on "sixty-two" and "sixty-four" with rolling labels, then a +2.5 cm "overnight" step and the 63-day bracket. I added staircase levels so both the lyric's 62.7 → 64.5 and the 2.5 cm step have a place. The baby walks behind the pen, hops up each step and grows. The "terrible" circle with "?", the growth-free hatching, and the front page with the struck "measurement error!" bubble appear only when their lines are sung. Checked at 80.3, 84.6, 86.5, 89.25; radio at 81.0, 86.5, 89.25.
  • Adolph (Fig. 8): "six degrees" is sung only once, so I paced the ratchet instead: 14 clicks of 6° evenly from "raised", landing on 84° at "drop". It has a protractor, a turning ratchet wheel and a readout box. Grant appears only when his line is sung, as an ink toddler on a dotted arc with "*" and "caught by an experimenter". The counters roll to 2,368 /h and 17. On "Crawling" the slope comes down and the baby bum-shuffles into its icon slot. In the radio cut the window absorbs Hirsh-Pasek's slot (to 107.92 s) and the baby simply holds there. Checked at 91.2, 92.5, 93.8, 94.6, 96.5, 99.4, 101.6, 102.6; radio at 92.6, 94.0, 99.8, 107.85.
  • Hirsh-Pasek (Fig. 9, absent from the radio cut): two speakers, each with a waveform envelope. The left one splits at the clause boundary and gets a tick; the right one splits mid-clause and gets a cross. The baby's head turns to the left one on "turn". It returns immediately if called with no lines; in the radio cut it has no window, as expected. Checked at 103.3, 104.7, 106.5, 107.9.
  • Saffran (Fig. 10; Fig. 9 on the radio cut): the syllable stream scrolls left from a speaker. The arcs go full ink and read 1.0 inside words on "one", and go light dashed and read .33 across word edges on "three". The readout reaches 2:00 and 45 reps.
